iḷaṅkumaraṉār
tamiḻvaḷam
26
1. kaḻaka amaiccar tiruvaraṅkar varalāṟu
āciriyar :
mutu muṉaivar irā.iḷaṅkumaraṉ

vaḷavaṉ patippakam
ceṉṉai - 600 017.


nūṟ kuṟippu
nūṟpeyar	:	iḷaṅkumaraṉār - tamiḻvaḷam 26
āciriyar	:	irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ
patippāḷar	:	kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
patippu	:	2013
tāḷ	:	16 ki veḷḷaittāḷ
aḷavu	:	1/8 temmi
eḻuttu	:	11 puḷḷi
pakkam	:	16 + 168 = 184
nūl kaṭṭamaippu	:	iyalpu (cātāraṇam)
vilai	:	urupā. 115/-
paṭikaḷ	:	1000

veḷiyīṭu
vaḷavaṉ patippakam
eṇ : 2 ciṅkāra vēlar teru,
tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉai - 600 017.
tolaipēci : 24339030


patippurai
ilakkiya ilakkaṇac cemmal mutumuṉaivar ayyā iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷ eḻupattaintu āṇṭu niṟaivu eytiyataip pōṟṟum vakaiyil tamiḻ iḷaiyarkkeṉṟu avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷaiyellām cērttu eḻupattaintu nūlkaḷ kaṭanta 2005 - ām āṇṭu tiruccit tirunakaril veḷiyiṭṭu em patippup paṇikkup perumai cērttuk koṇṭōm. 
ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñar eḻutiya paṭaippukaḷai 20 tokutikaḷāka (1 mutal 20 varai) avariṉ 81-ām āṇṭu niṟaivaiyoṭṭi kaṭanta 2009 ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭṭup perumai cērttōm. perumaikkup perumai cērkkum vakaiyil avar tam aṟivu viḷaiccalai 21 mutal 40 varai 
20 tokutikaḷai  83-ām āṇṭu (2012) niṟaivaiyoṭṭi tamiḻulakam payaṉkoṇṭu ceḻikkum vakaiyil veḷiyiṭukiṟōm. avar tam aruntamiḻp paṇiyai vaṇaṅki makiḻkiṟōm.
ipperumakaṉ vāḻum kālattilēyē ivar eḻutiya eḻuttukkaḷ, pēciya pēccukkaḷ aṉaittaiyum tokuttu veḷiyiṭuvatu eṉpatu tamiḻnūl patippu varalāṟṟil pōṟṟi makiḻvataṟkāṉa,  aṉaivarum piṉpaṟṟuvataṟkāṉa arumperum tamiḻp paṇiyākum.
ivar tannalam karutāmal tamiḻ nalam kāttu varupavar.  tammaimuṉṉiṟuttātu  tamiḻai muṉṉiṟuttum peruntamiḻaṟiñar.  tamakkeṉa vāḻātu tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻpavar. orunāḷiṉ muḻup
poḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻum tamiḻp pōrāḷi. caṅkac cāṉṟōr varicaiyil vaittu eṇṇattakka aruntamiḻaṟiñar. tamiḻ ilakkaṇa - ilakkiya marapaik kāttu varum marapu vaḻi aṟiñar. āravāram mikunta iṉṟaiya cūḻnilaiyil paṭāṭōpam iṉṟiyum, viḷamparap pōlimai iṉṟiyum,   tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ āḻa akalaṅkaḷai akaḻntu kāṭṭum toltamiḻaṟiñar. moḻinūl katiravaṉ pāvāṇariṉ vērccol āyvil avar kāṭṭiya vaḻiyil tam tamiḻāyvait  toṭarpavar. 
tamiḻāyvuk kaḷattil  tam āyvuppayaṇattait toṭaṅkum tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷukkum, tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷukkum, māṇavac celvaṅkaḷukkum taṅkat taṭṭil vaittup poṟ
kuviyalākat  tantuḷḷōm. em tamiḻ nūl patippup payaṇattil,  tamiḻtteṉṟal tiru.vi.ka.vaiyum, taṉittamiḻiyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷaiyum, moḻinūl katiravaṉ pāvāṇaraiyum, tamiḻ moḻikkāvalar ilakkuvaṉāraiyum vaḻikāṭṭiyākak koṇṭu tamiḻnūl parappiṉ ellaiyaik kaṇṭu kāṭṭum caṅkattamiḻc cāṉṟōrāka viḷaṅkum aiyā iḷaṅkumaraṉār  vāḻum kālattilēyē avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭuvatai yām peṟṟa pēṟākak karutukiṟōm. tamiḻar illamtōṟum irukkattakka ivvaruntamiḻc celvaṅkaḷait tamiḻ iḷam talaimuṟaikku vaippākak koṭuttuḷḷōm. 
 calukai pōṉāl pōkaṭṭum - eṉ 
	aluval pōṉāl pōkaṭṭum 
	talaimuṟai orukōṭi kaṇṭa - eṉ
	tamiḻ viṭutalai ākaṭṭum.
pāvēntar varikaḷai niṉaivil koḷvōm.
- patippāḷar.




peṟum pēṟu
vaḷam eṅkē irukkiṟatu?
vaḷam eṅkēyum irukkiṟatu! aḵtu illāta iṭamē illai! 
vaḷam eṅkum iruppatai eppaṭi aṟiyalām?
vaḷattait tēṭum uḷam iruntāl, vaḷam eṅkēyum iruppatai aṟiyalām?
vaḷattiṉ aḷavu eṉṉa?
vaḷattiṉ aḷavum uḷattiṉ aḷavēyām!
uḷattil vaḷam illaiyāṉāl, uḷḷa vaḷamum uṭaiyavaṉukkum utavātu ūrukkum ulakukkum utavātu!
vaḷattait tarum uḷattai oruvar vāykkap peṟṟāl pōtum!
avvaḷam kuṉṟiyaḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     kuṉṟattaṉaiya vaḷamāṉāl eṉṉa?
     tiṉai aḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     paṉai aḷavāṉāl eṉṉa?
     vaḷam vaḷamē!
tēṉī vaḷam? tiṉaittaṉai aḷavup pūvil, tēṉī koḷḷum tēṉ evvaḷavu irukkum?
kōttumpi koṇarum tēṉ vaḷam evvaḷavu irukkum?
kōttumpi koṇarvatē vaḷam, tēṉī koṇarvatu vaḷamillai eṉa evarāvatu kūṟuvārā?
vaḷam vaḷamē! avaravar uḻaippu, aṟivuttiṟam, uṇmaiyaṟivu, paṭṭaṟivu, kāla-iṭa-cūḻal koṭai- ākiyavaṟṟaip poṟuttatu atu.
poruḷ maṭṭumā vaḷam?
pulamai vaḷamillaiyā?
ūkkutal utavutal uṟutuṇaiyātal oṉṟutal āyavai - vaḷamillaiyā?
ellām vaḷamē! mallal vaḷaṉē eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. (uri.7) uṭalālum vaḷam; uḻaippālum vaḷam; uṇarvālum vaḷam; uraiyālum vaḷam; vaḷam paṭa vāḻa vēṇṭātār evar? vaḷam peṟa vēṇṭātār evar?
vaḷantalai mayaṅkiya vañci muṟṟam
eṉpatu cilampu! vaḷattiṉ aḷavu, aḷantu kaṭai aṟiyāvaḷam eṉpatu (25:33-34)
nāṭeṉpa nāṭā vaḷattaṉa
eṉpatu nāṭṭiṉ ilakkaṇam! eḷimai, iṉimai, iṟaimai, niṟaimai eṉa makkaḷ vāḻnta nāḷ ilakkaṇam! iṉṟō,
nāṉila vaḷaṅkaḷaiyum nāṭā nāṭillai!
enta nāṭu ēṟṟumati ceyyavillai?
enta nāṭu iṟakkumati ceyyavillai?
pālaivaḷattai nāṭiccōlai niṟṟal veḷippaṭai eṉṟāl, colvāṉēṉ?
nāṭāvaḷattil kuppaikkīrai cērātā? kuṟuntūṟu cērātā?
vaḷattai vaḻaṅkutal tāṉ piṟavik kaṭaṉē yaṉṟi aḷaviṭum poṟuppu vaḻaṅkuvārkku illai! peṟuvār poṟuppu. āṉāl,
vaḷam peṟṟup peṟṟup peruki vāḻntavar, avvaḷattait tamakku vaḻaṅkiya maṇṇukku maṟittu vaḻaṅkip peṟṟa kaṭaṉait tīrkkāmal pōṉāl kaṭaṉkārarākavē pōvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi, piṟavi aṭaiyāḷamē illāmalum pōyviṭuvār!
peṟṟavar vaḻiyākak karuvilē peṟṟa tiru eṉṉa! kaṟṟa kalviyāl peṟṟa vaḷam eṉṉa! paṭṭaṟivāl - āḻmaṉa āyvāl - tēṭik koṇṭa vaḷaṅkaḷ eṉṉa eṉṉa! piñcup paruva mutal perum pirivu varai peṟṟavaṟṟai, annilaipparuvattarkkup paruvamaḻai pōlap palappala vakaiyālum vaḻaṅkutal vāḻvār vāḻvuc cīrmaiyātal vēṇṭum!
purivu terinta nāḷ mutal pulamai perukivarum aḷavukkut takat-takat tolkāppiyar āṇai vaḻi,
ōytal āytal niḻattal cāay
         āvayiṉ nāṉkum uḷḷataṉ nuṇukkam
eṉṉum nālneṟik kaṭaṉkaḷaiyum niṟaivēṟṟal vēṇṭum! kūṟṟaiyum āṭal koḷḷum koḷkai ituveṉa uṇarvār ōyār, oḻiyār; cāyār; carintum pōkār!
eṉ iḷantaip paruvamē, āciriyap paruvamākiviṭṭatu.  akkālac cūḻal atu.
piṟantanāḷ : 30.01.1930
āciriyap paṇi ēṟṟa nāḷ: 08.04.1946
kāl caṭṭai pōṭum māṇavapparuvattil vēṭṭi kaṭṭum āciriyaṉāki viṭṭēṉ! mūṉṟāṇṭu āciriyap paṇi ceytāl pōtum! pulavar tērvait taṉiyāka eḻutalām eṉṉum vāyppu! avvāyppaic cikkeṉap paṟṟiyamaiyāl 1949, 1950, 1951 ākiya mūvāṇṭukaḷil toṭarntu nuḻaivut tērvu, pulavar iṭainilait tērvu, pulavar niṟai nilait tērvu eṉac ciṟappuṟa veṟṟi vāyppu eytiyatu.  uyarpaḷḷi, mēlpaḷḷi eṉpavaṟṟilum palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻiyal āyvuk kaḷattilumāka ēṟattāḻa 43 āṇṭukaḷ tamiḻvāḻvāka vāḻa vāyttatu.  tamiḻttoṇṭum, tamiḻneṟit toṇṭum eṉṟum viṭutal aṟiyā viruppoṭu ceytu koṇṭiruttalē iyaṟkaiyōṭu iyainta vāḻvāki viṭṭatu!
pulamait tērvukkuriya pāṭattaḷavilō, nūlkaḷiṉ aḷavilō eṉ kalvi niṉṟatu illai! tolkāppiyam mutal iṟṟaināḷ nūlkaḷ varai iyaṉṟa aḷavāl tamiḻvaḷam peṟutalai nōṉpākak koṇṭu kaṟkum pēṟupeṟṟēṉ.  pērāciriyar ci.ilakkuvaṉār, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar, nāvalar ca.cō.pāratiyār, varalāṟṟuc cemmal aracamāṇikkaṉār, paintamiḻp pāvalar m.ki.guªjhkdh®, uraivēntar auvai cu.turaicāmiyār, mūtaṟiñar cemmal va,cupa.māṇikkaṉār, tāmaraic celvar va,cuppaiyaṉār, īrōṭu vēlā, kuḻittalai mī.cu. iḷamuruku poṟcelvi, patippuc cemmal meyyappaṉār, pārati cō.cāminātaṉār, tamiḻppōrāḷi iḷavaḻakaṉār, tamiḻ mīṭpukkut talainiṉṟa āṉārūṉā, pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷaiyar kōvalaṅkaṇṇaṉār, malaiyaka māriyappaṉār iṉṉar nerukkat toṭarpukaḷ vāyttaṉa.
iḷantaip paruvattilēyē pāvēntar, ōki cuttāṉanta pāratiyār, kavimaṇiyār, kavirāca paṇṭita cekavīra
pāṇṭiyaṉār, aṟiñar mu.va. āyōr ariya kāṭciyum ciṉmuṟaic cantippukaḷum eṉṉuḷ pacumai nalkiṉa. 
tamiḻp poḻivukaḷum, āyvuk kaṭṭuraikaḷum, kuṭumpac caṭaṅkukaḷum eṉ mīṭṭeṭuppup paṇikaḷ āyiṉa!
em kuṭumpak kaṭamaikaḷ em paṇiyaiyō toṇṭaiyō kavarntu koḷḷā vakaiyil iṉiya tuṇaiyum makkaḷum amaintaṉar.
ataṉāl, kālamellām kaṟkavum kaṟpikkavum nūl
paṭaikkavum vāytta vāyppup peritāyiṟṟu.
aṉpum aṟivum eṉṉum nūl toṭaṅkic centamiḻc coṟporuḷ kaḷañciyam eṉat toṭarntu ēṟattāḻa 400 nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟavum veḷiyiṭavum vāyttaṉa.
kuḻantai nūlkaḷ, nūlaka nūlkaḷ, āyvu nūlkaḷ, akarātikaḷ, urainūlkaḷ, patippu nūlkaḷ, pā nūlkaḷ, kāppiyaṅkaḷ, kataikaḷ, kaṭṭuraikaḷ, tokuppukaḷ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ eṉap palappala tiṟattaṉavāyp pāl vāyp paruvattar mutal palkalaik kaḻaka āyvar varaikkum payaṉ koḷḷum vakaiyil avai amaintaṉa.
etai eṭuttālum pāṭalākkal orukālam; kataiyum nāṭakamum ākkal orukālam; patippup paṇiyē oru kālam; urai
kāṇalē oru kālam; paṭippaṭiyē collāyvē vāḻveṉa ākiviṭṭa kālam eṉa amaintaṉa.
ik koṭaikaḷ vaḻaṅkiyavai pārati patippakam, caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, maṇi patippakam, murukaṉ patippakam, vēlā patippakam, maṇivācakar patippakam, vēmaṉ patippakam, mūvēntar patippakam, pāvāṇar aṟakkaṭṭaḷai, cākittiya akātemi, ulakat tamiḻ ārāycci niṟuvaṉam, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam,  amiḻtam patippakam, vaḷavaṉ patippakam, māṇavar patippakam, amiḻtamaṇi patippakam, tiruvaḷḷuvar tavaccālai aṟakkaṭṭaḷai veḷiyīṭukaḷ eṉa virintaṉa.
nūlurup peṟṟu accaka vaḻiyum patippaka vaḻiyum kāṇappeṟāmalum etirpārā maṟaivukaḷālum ēṟattāḻac ciṟitum peritumāy oḻintavai irupatukku mēl āyiṉa.
centamiḻ, centamiḻc celvi, tamiḻppāvai, kuṟaḷ
neṟi, kuṟaḷiyam, malarkaḷ, āyvaraṅkaṅkaḷ eṉak kaṭṭuraikaḷ perukiṉa.  accukku varātavaiyum ākiṉa!  ivai eṉṟum orumukamāyk kiṭaikka vāyppillai.
em nūlkaḷ emmiṭamē muppatukkumēl illāmal maṟaintaṉa.  eḻutiyaṉa ellāvaṟṟaiyum orumukamākap peṟap
palar avāviṉār. tavattiru āttumāṉanta aṭikaḷār (āruyirkku aṉpar) atil talaippaṭṭu niṉṟār.  āyar āṇṭakai cūcaimāṇikkaṉār cuṭarēṟṟiṉār. pāratitācaṉ palkalaikkaḻakam matippuṟu mutumuṉaivar paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiyatu.  ataṟku makiḻvuṟṟa vakaiyāl pāvāṇar tamiḻiyakkam mutalām amaippukaḷum, muṉaivar tirumāṟaṉār mutalām ārvalarkaḷum viḻā oṉṟu eṭuttaṉar.  avviḻāt talaimaiyait tiruttaku tuṇaivēntar poṉṉavaikkō ēṟṟār.  tavattiru āttumāṉanta aṭikaḷār vāḻtturaittār.  poṟiñar tamiḻaṟiñar pālakaṅkātaraṉār eṉ nūlkaḷ cilavaṟṟai oḷippaṭiyākki uyaraṭṭaik kaṭṭil vaḻaṅki avaṟṟai muḻutuṟap peṟa mīḷacciṭutal vēṇṭum eṉṟār.  tamiḻākkap patippē vāḻvāka oṉṟiya iḷavaḻakaṉār muḻutuṟa acciṭa oppiṉār.  aṭikaḷār veḷiyīṭṭup poṟuppu ēṟpatākak kūṟiṉār. kiṭaikkum nūlkaḷ tavirttup piṟavaṟṟaiyellām acciṭum tiṭṭam koṇṭu ēṟattāḻa irupatu, irupatu tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭuvatu eṉa uṟutippaṭuttap paṭṭatu.  avvakaiyil vāyppatē, iḷaṅkumaraṉār tamiḻvaḷam eṉṉum it tokai nūlkaḷām! it tokaiyil viṭupaṭum nūlkaḷ kaṭṭuraikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ palapala;  tokukka vāykkā vakaiyil kiṭṭāvakaiyil maṟappu vakaiyil viṭupāṭuṭaiyavai uḷa.  eṉiṉum toṇṇuṟu viḻukkāṭēṉum oru mukamākap peṟum pēṟu vāykkiṉṟatu eṉpatu makiḻvūṭṭuvatām!
makkaḷ vāḻvu vaḷarnilaiyatu! nūlkaḷum vaḷar nilaiyavai! ātalāl oruvāḻvuk koṭai maṭṭumaṉṟu ītu; oruvāḻvu peṟat takka palparuvak koṭaiyumām eṉal cālum! itaṉ aṭippaṭaiyām ettēṭalilum peritum tōyāmal tamiḻvaḷat tēṭalilēyē kālamellām cōmpalaic cuṭṭerittu, iṭukkaṇukku iṭukkaṇ ākki vāḻnta vāḻvuk koṭaiyām itu!
ivvaḷattai muḻutuṟa eṇṇiṉ oṉṟu uṟutiyāka veḷippaṭum.  nalavāḻvu nalvāḻvu karutāta - iyaṟkai iṟaimaip potumai karutāta - eppaṭaippum empaṭaippil irā eṉpatēyām.  ivaṟṟaip payilvār peṟum pēṟum atuvāka amaiyiṉ appēṟē yām peṟum pēṟām.
ivvaḷam veḷivara virumpiṉōr uḻaittōr utaviṉōr āya aṉaivarkkum naṉṟik kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉ! vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
nellukku umiyuṇṭu
nīrukku nuraiyuṇṭu
pullitaḻ pūvukkum uṇṭu
kuṟaikaḷaintu niṟai peytu
kaṟṟal, kaṟpōr kaṭaṉeṉal muntaiyar muṟai
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ,
irā.iḷaṅkumaraṉ

mukavurai
ṭākṭar pā. naṭarācaṉ
kaḻaka amaiccar tiruvaraṅkar varalāṟu eṉpatu, avar vāḻnta kālattiya tamiḻmoḻi, caivattiṉ varalāṟu eṉak kūṟalām. kaṟṟōrkaḷ, tamiḻil pēcutal tamakkuc ciṟappaḷikkā teṉak karutiya akkālattu ivar tāymoḻippaṟṟiṉ mikutiyāl, ammoḻiyām tamiḻukkuk kaḻakam kaṇṭu, pakalum iravum ayarātu pāṭupaṭṭup pārellām pōṟṟa, ak kaṉṉimoḻiyiṉ vaḷam perukki vaḷarcci kaṇṭavar. ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam, camayam, varalāṟu, tiṟaṉāyvu, moḻipeyarppu, icaittamiḻ vaḷarcci, nāṭakattamiḻp perukkam pōṉṟa iṉṉum palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷil nūlkaḷ toṭarntuvara, tam iḷaiyavar tiru.va.cuppaiyāpiḷḷai avarkaḷukku vaḻi kāṭṭiyāy iruntavar tiruvaraṅkarēyāvar.
ivar neñcuram mikkavar. taḷarāta muyaṟciyum caliyāta uḻaippum koṇṭavar. vāḻviṉ uyariya kuṟikkōḷkaḷait teḷivākat tērntu kāṇum iyalpiṉar; iṉiya collālum, itam mikka naṭattaiyālum paḻakiyavar neñcaṅkaḷil nilaiyāṉa iṭampeṟṟavar. kuṭumpattaiyum, cārntavaraiyum pēṇum kuṇanalam vāynta cemmal. tamiḻmoḻiyiṉpālum, caivattiṉpālum ivarukkirunta āḻamāṉa īṭupāṭu ivarai aṉaivarum pōṟṟum arumaṇiyāka - cuṭarmaṇiyāka ākkiṟṟu.
ciṉṉañciṟu paruvattil tam tantaiyār tiru. vayiramuttup piḷḷai avarkaḷai iḻanta tiruvaraṅkarum, ivar tam uṭaṉpiṟantōrum taṉ aṉṉaiyār cuntarattammaiyār aravaṇaippilēyē vāḻavēṇṭiyavarkaḷāyiṉar. vaṟumai
yiṉ tākkutalāl tiruvaraṅkar kaṟṟuvanta kalvikkut taṭaiyēṟ
paṭṭatu. ētēṉum paṇiceytu poruḷīṭṭa viḻainta ivar, tūttukkuṭi vantu ciṟṟāḷ vēlaiyil īṭupaṭṭār. aṅku ivarukku vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntarampiḷḷai eṉpavar toṭarpu ēṟpaṭṭatu. ip periyār ciṟanta civaneṟiyāḷar, tavattiru maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tiṟaṉ aṟinta cemmal; aṭikaḷāriṭam pēraṉpum perumatippum koṇṭavar; tūttukkuṭi caiva cittānta capaiyil īṭupāṭum ivarukkiruntatu. ivaratu tuṇaiyum tūttukkuṭit taṅkalum tiruvaraṅkar vāḻvil perum tiruppattai ēṟpaṭuttiṉa. eṉiṉum tam vāḻvil takka poruḷvaḷam ēṟpaṭavillaiyē eṉṟa kavalai ivarukkiruntatu. eṉavē ivar tūttukkuṭiyil nilaittirukka virumpavillai. vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntaraṉār ālōcaṉaiyiṉpaṭi koḻumpu ceṉṟār.
aṅku oru vaṇika niṟuvaṉattil kaṇakkeḻutum paṇiyil amarnta tiruvaraṅkaṉār tam uyariya paṇpukaḷālum, ūṟṟeṉac curantu poṅkum tamiḻp paṟṟu, caivappaṟṟukaḷālum koḻumpu vaṇikap perumakkaḷiṭam mikka nerukkamuṭaiyava
rāṉār.
tavattiru maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāraik kuṟittu ēṟkaṉavē kēḷvippaṭṭirunta tiruvaraṅkar, mēlum avartam ciṟappiyalpu kaḷai aṟintu, avarait tam uḷḷattil nilainiṟutti vaḻipaṭṭu vantār. aṭikaḷārai īḻattukkaḻaittum payaṉpeṟa eṇṇiṉār. ivaratu viruppattukku icaivaḷittaṉar aṅkuḷḷa tamiḻpperumakkaḷ,
irumuṟai tavattiru maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārai ilaṅkaikku varavaḻaittu, centamiḻccelvam paṟṟiyum, caivaviḷakkam kuṟittum aṅkuḷḷa paṟpala ūrkaḷil coṟpōrukku nikaḻtta vaittār tiruvaraṅkar aṭikaḷār iṉiya ariya uraimaṇi
kaḷaik kēṭkum makkaḷellām āṉantakkaṭalil mūḻkiṉar. itaṟku vāyppaḷitta tiruvaraṅkarai vaṇikapperumakkaḷum ēṉaiya cāṉṟōrum pārāṭṭip pukaḻntaṉar.
tiruvaraṅkar ceyta maṟṟoru arumperum ceyalaiyum iṅkuk kuṟippiṭavēṇṭum. aṭikaḷāriṉ poruḷ tēvaiyai uṇarntu pala perumakkaḷiṭam paṇam tiraṭṭi avarukku utaviṉār ivar. itu eḷitāṉa ceyalō? yāvarālum ceyyatakkatō? ivvutavi aṭikaḷārukku mikka payaṉai aḷittatu. avar toṭaṅkiyirunta māḷikaip paṇi niṟaivēṟuvataṟkum, putiya accakam toṭaṅkuvataṟkum, avaratu aṟivuk kaṭal itaḻaic cemmaiyāy naṭattuvataṟkum payaṉpaṭṭatu. mēlum aṭikaḷārai ūkki arumperum nūlkaḷ ākkavum, āyvuppaṇikaḷaic cevvaṉē ceyyavum vāyppaḷittatu. tiruvaraṅkariṉ ivvarum utaviyai aṭikaḷārē naṉṟip pēruṇarcciyil pala kaṭitaṅkaḷ eḻutip pārāṭṭiṉā reṉṟāl vēṟu eṉṉa vēṇṭum!
ilaṅkaiyiliruntu tāyakam tirumpiya tiruvaraṅkar ceṉṉaikku vantu, aṅkut tam tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyait toṭaṅki naṭattiyatum, tavattiru maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār tamiḻp paṇi vaḷarcciyaik karuttil koṇṭēyākum. ceṉṉaiyil iruppiṉ aṭikaḷārōṭum neruṅkip paḻakavum avarukku avvappōtu utavavum vāyppuk kiṭaikkum eṉak karutiyē aṅkut taṅkalāṉār.
nellaiyil tiru. mā. tiraviyam piḷḷai eṉpavarōṭu tiruvaraṅkarum iṇaintu paṅkutārarkaḷaic cērttuttakka caṭṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷōṭu 1920il tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya caivacittānta nūṟpatippukkaḻakam limiṭeṭ eṉṟa niṟuvaṉattait toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ. iruvarum iṇaintē ip paṇiyaic ceytārkaḷeṉṟālum tiruvaraṅkar muyaṟciyum uḻaippumē itil mēlōṅki niṉṟaṉa. nellaiyilēyē taṅkik kaḻakappaṇiyai iṭaiyaṟātu ceytu koṇṭiruntār tiruvaraṅkar.
ivarukku illattuṇaiviyāy vāyttavar maṟaimalai aṭikaḷāriṉ tirumakaḷār nīlāmpikai ammaiyār āvar. niṟainta tamiḻppulamai vāynta ivvammaiyār tam tantaiyārukkē taṉittamiḻ eḻucciyait tōṟṟuvittā
reṉṟāl ivaratu taṉittamiḻ ārvattai evvāṟu pukaḻvatu! aṭikaḷār taṉittamiḻ iyakkam kaṇṭu ataṉvaḻi nūlkaḷ pala pārttu veḷikkoṇarntatil ivvammaiyārukku mikunta paṅkuṇṭu. ammaiyār eḻuti veḷivantuḷḷa taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷum, vaṭacoṟṟamiḻ akarātiyum tamiḻukkuk kiṭaitta vairamaṇip pēḻaikaḷ! ivvammaiyār ālōcaṉaikaḷum tiruvaraṅkarukku mikunta payaṉ aḷittirukka vēṇṭum!
tiruvaraṅkar oru taṉimaṉitarāka niṉṟu tam tiṟaṉālum muyaṟciyālum āṟṟiya paṇikaḷ namakku viyappait tarukiṉṟaṉa. ippaṇikaḷiliruntu tamiḻmoḻi mītum, caivattiṉ mītum ivarukkirunta āḻamāṉa paṟṟiṉai uṇarntu koḷḷalām. teṉṉintiya caivacittānta caṅkam, tirukkuṟaḷ tērvukaḷ, tirukkuṟṟālattil cāral mānāṭu, nellait tiruneṟit toṇṭar kuḻām, teṉṉintiya tamiḻccaṅkam, meykaṇṭa cāttira mānāṭu, icaittamiḻkkaḻakam pōṉṟa ettaṉaiyō amaippukaḷum nikaḻccikaḷum ivaratu īṭu iṇaiyaṟṟa tiṟamaiyai veḷippaṭuttum.
tam iḷaiyavariṉ tiṟaṉaiyum ceytoḻil nērttiyaiyum ciṟanta kuṟikkōḷ teḷivaiyum naṉkaṟintavar tiruvaraṅkar. ceṉṉaiyil kaḻakattiṉ kiḷaiyamaippu oṉṟait toṭaṅki ap poṟuppait tam iḷaiyavariṭamē (tiru. va.cu. piḷḷaiyiṭam) oppuvittār.
kaḻakam toṭaṅkiya kālattiliruntu aḵtu āṇṭukku āṇṭu mikunta nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭut tam vaḷarcciyiṉaik kāṭṭiyatu. toṭakkakālak kaḻaka veḷiyīṭukaḷē kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭaiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkiṉa. mēlaināṭṭiliruntu veḷivantu koṇṭirunta nūlkaḷukkoppat tamiḻil accut teḷivōṭum, aḻakiya kaṭṭukkōppōṭum, nāṭṭukkum moḻikkum nalam tarum ciṟanta nūlkaḷaik kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭu vantatu. taram tāḻnta nūlkaḷaiyō, tam kuṟikkōḷukku māṟupaṭṭa nūlkaḷaiyō kaḻakam orupōtum veḷiyiṭṭa tillai. ivvamaippu nūṉmuṟai, aṉṟumutal iṉṟuvarai aṉaivarālum pārāṭṭappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. kaḻakap patippu eṉṟāl atu ciṟanta patippu eṉṟa poruḷait tarumpaṭiyāka nūlkaḷ amaintu viḷaṅkiṉa. atuvē kaḻakac ciṟappukkum kāraṇamāyiṟṟu.
ettaṉaiyō eḻuttāḷarkaḷ, pulavarkaḷ, kaviñarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷait tamiḻmoḻikku aṟimukam ceytu vaitta perumai, tiruvaraṅkarukkuṇṭu. puttam putiya nūlkaḷ, paḻaiya nūlkaḷukkup putturaikaḷ, kaṟṟōrēyaṉṟi maṟṟōrum kaṟṟup payaṉ aṭaiyattakka eḷiya urainaṭaip patippukaḷ, ciṟuvar nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavai veḷivarat tam tampiyārukku ivarē uṟutuṇaiyāka niṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭalāṉār.
1934l ceṉṉai mākāṇat tamiḻar mānāṭu naṭattavum, ceṉṉai mākāṇat tamiḻar caṅkam amaikkavum pēraṟiñarkaḷōṭu tiruvaraṅkarum, avar iḷaiyavar tiru. va.cu. piḷḷaiyum iṇaintu ceyalāṟṟiya paṇikaḷ kuṟippiṭat
takkavai. tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyaiyum, iṉimaiyaiyum pēṇu vataṟkum, piṟamoḻit tākkattiliruntu tamiḻaik kāppataṟkum, tamiḻp pātukāppuc caṅkam amaittu ataṉvaḻi aṟiñar perumakkaḷōṭu kaḻakattiṉ inta iraṭṭaiyarkaḷum cērntu ceyta paṇikaḷ maṟakkaṟpālaṉavalla. kaḻaka itaḻākiya centamiḻc celviyait tamiḻ vaḷarccip paṇikkākavē payaṉpaṭutti vantatai yāvarum aṟivar. ippaṭi ettaṉai ettaṉaiyō paṇikaḷ. kaḻakap paṇiyaiyē tam muḻunērap paṇiyākakkoṇṭu kaḻakattaik kaṭṭākak kāttup pēṇivanta tiruvaraṅkaṉārukkuttāṉ ettaṉai cōtaṉaikaḷ! avaruṭaṉ kaḻaka amaiccarkaḷāka iruntavarkaḷē avarukku etirāka māṟi avar mītu vēṇṭāta paḻiyaic cumattiṉārkaḷeṉṟāl itaṉiṉum tuṉpam tara vēṟu eṉṉa vēṇṭum? tammītu cumattiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷ aṉaittum tavaṟāṉavai eṉpataip paṅkāḷikaḷ kūṭṭattil takka cāṉṟukaḷōṭu ivar meyp pittuk kāṭṭiṉār. piṉpu aṉaivarum ivaratu nērmaiyai naṉku terintukoṇṭaṉar. attakaiya ceyal muṟaiyai aṉṟu ivar mēṟkoḷḷāviṭṭāl iṉṟu kaḻakam eṉṟa ōr amaippē iruntirukkātu. aṉṟiliruntu tiruvaraṅkar oruvarē paṅkāḷikaḷil nampikkaikkuriya amaiccarāy amarntu kaḻakappaṇiyāṟṟi varalāṉār. nīṇṭakāla naṇparkaḷum tiruvaraṅkarai naṉku aṟintavarkaḷum avarukku māṟupaṭṭu muraṇpaṭṭa ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭṭapōtu, avar neñcam kaṉṟippōṉatil viyappētum illaiyē!
kuṭumpattilum tuyaram tarum pala nikaḻccikaḷ toṭarntu naṭaipeṟṟaṉa. ōyvē illāta uḻaippum, mīṇṭum mīṇṭum vanta mōtiya tollaikaḷum, tuyaraṅkaḷum tiruvaraṅkar uṭal nalattiṟku ūṟu viḷaittaṉa. avartam uyiraiyē (1944il) kavarntaṉa. ām, avar iṉṟu illai. āṉāl avar ūṉṟiya vitaikaḷ muḷaittu vaḷarntu pala payaṉmaraṅkaḷ koṇṭa poḻilākik kaṉikaḷai utirkkiṉṟaṉa. ik kaṉikaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappākavum, tamiḻar vāḻviṉ nalamākavum, caivattiṉ uyarvākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kālamellām ik kaṉikaḷ utirntukoṇṭē irukkum.
vāḻvellām tamiḻ vaḷarccikkum, tamiḻar nalaṉukkum ayarātu uḻaitta oru māmētaiyiṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟṟai viṟuviṟuppum cuvaiyum koṇṭa iṉiya tamiḻnaṭaiyil eḻutiyuḷḷār pulavar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār. tamiḻ vaḷartta cemmaliṉ varalāṟu muḻumaiyum tamiḻmaṇam kamaḻkiṟatu. nūlaip paṭikkat toṭaṅkiyatum paṭittu muṭikkum varai uḷḷam vēṟu etilum cellāta nilaikku, iḵtu īrppuc cakti koṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. pala nikaḻccikaḷai nam kaṇmuṉ naṭappaṉapōl ōviyappaṭuttik kāṭṭum āciriyar tiṟaṉ pārāṭṭutaṟkuriyatu.
iv varalāṟṟaip paṭikkum vāyppup peṟṟōr iv varalāṟṟu nāyakaraippōl tāmum āka vēṇṭum eṉa avāk koḷḷutal iyalpu. nalluḷḷamum nayakkum kuṟikkōḷum koṇṭavarkaḷ potumaip paṇiyilaṉṟō ciṟappaik kāṇpar. ac ciṟappaiyaṉṟō vāḻkkaiyil tām peṟum uṇmaiyāṉa veṟṟi eṉṟum koḷvar? av veṟṟiyiṉai viḷakkik kāṭṭum oru ciṟanta nūlē itu.


76, āriṅṭaṉ cālai,		
ceṉṉai - 31.
(om) pā. naṭarācaṉ
6-6-82


FOREWORD
K. RAMALINGANAR, M.A.

The life history of Thiru. V. Tiruvarangam Pillai, entitled ‘Arangar’ is a valuable addition to the very few biographical works in Tamil. Tiruvarangam, shortened as ‘Arangar’, in this book, impressed me as a man of action, when I used to meet him now and then at Madras, before 1938. The love and reverance I had for the venerable Maraimalai Adigal and the fact that Tiruvarangam Pillai became the beloved Son-in-law of that great savant endeared Arangar to me.
In 1938, I was posted to Palayamcottai as Municipal Commissioner and Tiruvarangam Pillai became a close acquaitance of mine. The acquaitance grew into friendship and gradually into fraternity. We used to meet each other frequently. During my visits to his house, his spouse Neelambigai Tiruvarangam and his aged mother Sundaram were hospitable to me.
Tiruvarangam Pillai made his appearance outside dressed in a ‘dhoti’ with coat on and an upper garment over the latter. The coat had huge pockets bulging with all sorts of useful scripts and documents collected from knowledgeable persons and scholars of merit, whom he met while on his literary pursuits. I had never seen the pockets empty. When once he visited me in my house, I showed him the notebook in which I had copied my poems. He read them with avidity and expressed a desire to take the notebook with him so that the poems could be printed and published.
From my personal contacts with him and the impressions I gained therefrom, I can vouchsafe that a true and graphical account of his personality has been portrayed in this biography. Tiruvarangam Pillai’s passion for work cannot be better expressed effectively than what has been rendered by the biographer in the book under review. Irrespective of the venue of his activity-Tuticorin or Colombo, Madras or Tirunelveli-and avocation-clerk or partner of a company or an orginator and promoter of the South India Saiva Siddhanta Works Publishing Soceity, he deployed the same fervor.
The diction employed by the author in this biography is so attractive and the style classical, that I could brook no interruption, while absorbed in reading it. The language is chaste and rythmic and at the sametime strong and powerful depicting how Tiruvarangam Pillai met the arguments of those who opposed him, baffling them.
Arangar, as Tiruvarangam Pillai is affectionately called in this book, was undaunted, while not only those who differed from him generally, but also those, supposed to be close to him, picked holes in the performance of his duties relating to the Saiva Siddhanta Works Publishing Soceity. He was sure of his ground. There was nothing wrong or out of the way in what he did, but the defects attributed to his work were either baseless or invented by the jealous of him or due to faulty understanding and misinformation. He stood like a rock against odds and but for him, the South India Saiva Siddhanta Works Publishing Soceity would not have grown to such dimensional proportion, as it is today.
Arangar held strong views on the question of speaking and writing chaste Tamil, avoiding induction of words from Sanskrit and other languages into Tamil. He felt perfectly convinced that the Tamil language not only lost its natural form and charm by such an admixture, but also that such dilution of the language had given room for the false notion that Tamil was derived from Sanskrit. The dilution had paved the way for Kannada, Telugu, Malayalam and Tulu to break away from it as separate entities. Had not Sanskrit words forged their way into Tamil, these languages would not have sprung up and the South would be speaking one language today. He was not worried by the bogey of those, dubbing persons advocating the use of chaste Tamil as ‘Puritanic.’ Himself a firm believer in the use of pure Tamil and an advocate of the same, his devotion to the venerable Maraimalai Adigal and his marital relationship as the consort of his daughter Tirumathi Neelambigai Ammaiyar, made him more staunch in this respect.
One of the noteworthy achievements to his credit was the shaping of his younger brother Subbiah Pillai in his mould, the future Managing Director of the Publishing Soceity, which owes its superb and enviable position in the book publishing field to him. Arangar, a hard Taskmaster given a rigid training to his brother in the ways and methods of developing this publishing house. Subbiah Pillai, in turn, proved worthy of the confidence and brought name and fame to the Soceity by his strenuous efforts. He made it hum with activity and stands as monument to the memory of his brother ‘Arangar’.
The biographer deserves to be feliciated for the masterly way in which he has mobilised almost all the important facts of Arangar’s life and marshalled them in logical sequence, using forceful and inimitable language with flourish and fragrance. Even those evincing not much interest in the book as a biography, would get fascinated to read and re-read it for sheer enjoyment and the wonderful way in which the biographer had woven his fabric in beautiful prose.



nūlāciriyar nutalvu
				kaḻaka ilakkiyac cemmal
			pulavar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ			
				talaimait tamiḻāciriyar
				mu. mu. u. paḷḷi, maturai
kaḻaka āṭciyar tāmaraic celvar va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ maṇiviḻā 1957 il nikaḻntatu. appoḻutu kaḻakap pulavar tirumiku pa. irāmanāta piḷḷai avarkaḷ, kaḻaka āṭciyar avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟuc curukkam varaintār. piṉṉar va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ 75 ām āṇṭai niṟaivu kuṟitta pavaḷa viḻā 1973 il nikaḻntatu. appoḻutu veḷiyiṭappeṟṟa pavaḷaviḻā malaril avarkaḷ varalāṟṟaic caṟṟē virittu yāṉ eḻutiṉēṉ. piṉṉark, kaḻaka maṇiviḻā 1982 caṉavariyil nikaḻntatu. atu pōḻtu av varalāṟṟai mēlum yāṉ viritteḻutiṉēṉ. atu, 350 pakka aḷavil, arumai vāynta paṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ veḷippaṭṭatu. ākaliṉ, va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟu tokai, vakai, viri eṉṉum nūl vakai mūṉṟaiyum oruṅkē koḷva tāy amaintatu. viriyiṉ viriyum vēṇṭum eṉṉum viḻumiya viḻaivuṭaiya perumakkaḷum uḷar eṉpatai niṉaikka iṉimai mēlum eḻukiṉṟatu.
va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ varalāṟu veḷiyīṭṭukku aṇiyañ ceyyuṅkāl avarkaḷ uḷḷattil ōr uṟuttutal eḻuntatu. tamiyēṉ varalāṟṟu nūlaik kaḻaka maṇiviḻāvil veḷiyiṭuvatu paṟṟi eṉ niṉaivukku nēṟṟu vantapōtu eṉ arumait tamaiyaṉār avarkaḷiṉ ciṟappu mikka varalāṟṟu nūlaiyaṉṟō mutaṟkaṇ eḻutac ceytu veḷiyiṭutal vēṇṭum; piṉṉarē eṉatu varalāṟṟu nūlai veḷiyiṭuvatu poruttamāyirukkum eṉṟa eṇṇam eṉ maṉat takattē tōṉṟi varuttukiṉṟatu eṉṟu varunti eḻutiṉar (8-12-81).
atukāl eṉ iṭakkaṇṇiṉ karumaṇiyai oṭṭi vaḷarntirunta veṇpaṭalat tacaiyai aṟuvai maruttuvam ceytiruntēṉ. ataṉāl, taṅkaḷukku aṟuvai maruttuvam ceyyap peṟṟa kaṇ cevvitāka vēṇṭumē! eṉ ceyvatu! eṉ maṉattil ēṟpaṭṭa uṇarcciyai uṇmai aṉpuḷḷa taṅkaṭkut terivikkak kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷēṉ. ippoḻutu eḻuta muṭiyāviṭiṉum eṉatu karuttait taṅkaḷukkut terivikkak kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷēṉ. ataṟkut tāṅkaḷ kavalai koḷḷa vēṇṭā eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar.
16-12-81 il eṉ arumai aṇṇā avarkaḷ kaḻakam toṭaṅkiyatu mutal avarkaḷ vāḻnta kālam varaiyuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷ celvi toṭaṅkiyatu mutal 1944 varai veḷivanta cilampukaḷil kāṇalām. meykaṇṭa cāttira mānāṭṭai 7 nāḷkaḷ 1941 il naṭattiṉārkaḷ. atuvē avarkaḷ uṭalukkup  pērūṟāka ēṟpaṭṭatu. tirukkuṟṟālac cāral mānāṭu mūṉṟō nāṉkō naṭattiṉār. nellaiyil caivap periyar mānāṭu ciṟappāka naṭaipeṟṟatōṭu, teṉṉintiya cittānta caṅkattiṉ cārpil caiva mānāṭukaḷ naṭaipeṟṟaṉa eṉṟum iṉṉavāṟu pala kuṟippukaḷaic cuṭṭiṉar.
perumakaṉār va.cu. avarkaḷiṉ ārvattaiyum kaṭap
pāṭṭuṇarvaiyum eṇṇi makiḻnta yāṉ tiruvaraṅkar varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷait tokukkalāṉēṉ. iv varalāṟṟukkuk kiṭaitta karuvikaḷ palappala. avaṟṟuḷ kuṟippiṭattakkavai ēḻu. avai :
1.	centamiḻc celviyiṉ mutal irupattoru 
	cilampukaḷ.
2.	pērā. tirumiku maṟai. tirunāvukkaracu avarkaḷ 
	tiruvaraṅkarum nīlāmpikaiyārum iyaṟkai eytiya 
	kālaiyil varainta varalāṟṟuc curakka nūlkaḷum, 
	tavattiru. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār varalāṟṟup peru 
	nūlum.
3.	peruntiruvāṭṭi cuntarat tammaiyār pirivu kuṟittuc 
	centamiḻc celvi cilampu 27 il itaḻāciriyar 
	varainta varalāṟṟuk kuṟippukaḷ.
4.	maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ tiruvaraṅkarukku varainta
	kaṭitaṅkaḷum, tiruvaraṅkarum nīlāmpikaiyārum va. cu. 
	avarkaḷukkum piṟarkkum varainta kaṭitaṅkaḷum.
5.	kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭa palvēṟu malarkaḷilum tamiḻc 
	cāṉṟōrkaḷ varainta kuṟippukaḷ.
6.	va.cu. avarkaḷ avvappōtu varaintuḷḷa kuṟippu 
	kaḷum, nērilum viḻākkaḷilum nekkuruki niṉṟu 
	uraitta uraikaḷum.
7.	kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭa palvēṟu aṟikkaikaḷum ceytik 
	kuṟippukaḷum.
ik karuvikaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum vaḻaṅkiya perumakkaḷukku iv varalāṟṟu nūl āciriyaṉ eṉṉum nilaiyil naṉṟi kūṟum kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉ.
iv varalāṟṟuk kaippaṭi nilaiyil paṭittum paṭikkak kēṭṭum urukiya perumakkaḷ iruvar. avarkaḷ va. cu. avar kaḷum maṟai. tirunāvukkaracu avarkaḷum āvar. araṅkaroṭu muṉṉavarum ampikaiyāroṭu piṉṉavarum uṭaṉ piṟappurimai uṭaiyar allarō! ākaliṉ ivarkaḷ uṭaṉ piṟantu uṭaṉ vaḷarntu, nīruṭaṉāṭic cīruṭaṉ peruki ōluṭaṉāṭṭap pāluṭaṉuṇṭu, palluṭaṉeḻuntu colluṭaṉ kaṟṟup paḻamaiyum payiṟṟiyum paṇpum naṇpum viḻuppamum oḻukkamum māṭciyum uṭaiyavar eṉṟu iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uraikārar niral peṟavuraikkum uraittiṟa uṇmaiyai meyppittu eṉṉaiyum urukkiṉar.
amaiccar araṅkar vaḷḷuvar vakutta amaiccar tiṟaṅkaḷellām oruṅkē amaintavar. amaiccarukku vaḷḷuvar vakukkum tiṟaṅkaḷ irupatām. avai : ceyalukkuriya karuvi ceytaṟkām kālam, ceyyum vakai, ceyyum aruñceyal añcāmai, naṟkuṭip piṟappu, kākkum tiṟaṉ, kaṟṟaṟinta aṟivu, ayarā muyaṟci, vēṇṭāraip pirittal, vēṇṭuvārai iṇaittal, pirintu vantāraip piṇaittal, āyntu terital, tērntu ceytal, oru talaiyākat tuṇital, aṟaṉ aṟital, āṉṟamainta collal, tiṟaṅkaṇṭaṟital, nūlaṟivu, nuṇṇaṟivu eṉpaṉa ivvirupatum tiruvaraṅkarukkup peruvaraṅkaḷāka vāyttavai eṉpatai iv varalāṟṟaik kaṟpār eḷitil aṟintu koḷvar.
oru varalāṟu curuṅkiyatā? virintatā? ivaṟṟaik karutuvatu mutaṉmaiyaṉṟām. varalāṟṟiṉ nōkkam niṟai vēṟiyuḷḷatā eṉpatē mutaṉmaiyāṉa nōkkamām. annōkkam in nūlil niṟaivuṟuttap peṟṟuḷatu eṉṟē nampukiṉṟēṉ.
ēṟattāḻa irupattaintu āṇṭukaḷāka eṉ vāḻvai muṟṟilum tamiḻ vāḻvāka ākki, eḷḷattaṉaip poḻutum payaṉiṉṟi oḻiyāmal iṉiya tamiḻp paṇi ceytaṟkut tiruvaruḷ tuṇaiyāl vāytta peruntakai tāmaraic celvar 
va. cuppaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷ āvar. avarkaḷukku in nūṟpaṇi nōkkiyum naṉṟi kūṟuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭaiyēṉ.

celvam, tirunakar
maturai - 6.
tamiḻt toṇṭaṉ, 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.
26-4-82



1. pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟiya kaḻakam
tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka eṉpatu poyyā moḻi. maṉitat tōṟṟattiṟkurittāy amainta im moḻi, ōr amaippiṉ tōṟṟattiṟkum urimai pūṇṭatēyām pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟiya pulamai maintar pērārvattāl pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟiya pulamait tiruviṉar tuṇaiyāl tōṉṟiyatu teṉṉintiya caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam ākum.
kaḻakattiṟku itupōḻtu (1980) maṇiviḻā. atu tōṉṟiya āṇṭu 1920. aṟupāṉ āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟiya kaḻakam āṟṟiyuḷḷa paṇikaḷ poṉṉeḻuttukaḷil poṟikkat takka perumai uṭaiyatām. ataṉai āytaṟku oru neṟi muṟaiyai vakuttut tiṟaṉuṟat tantuḷḷaṉar, tērccimikka tiruvāḷarkaḷ. am muṟai etirmaṟai muṟaiyām!
tiru. piḷḷaiyarkaḷaik kāṇumpōtellām, niṉaikkum pōtellām avarkaḷatu acaikkalākāta aṉṉai moḻip paṟṟum taṇikkalākāta taṉittamiḻ ārvamum eṉ neñcattai īrttu iṉpuṟuttum. tamiḻttāy ceyta tavappayaṉāy avarkaḷ tamaiyaṉār tiru. va. tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷum tiru. piḷḷaiyavarkaḷum aimpatu āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu, tirunelvēlit teṉṉintiya caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattait tōṟṟuvittirāmal iruntāl, tōṟṟuvittum ōyātu muṉaintu iṉṟuḷḷa arumperu nilaikkuk kaḻakam uyarumāṟu uḻaittirāmal iruntāl, ittaṉai ilakkiya ilakkaṇa aṟivu cāl nūlkaḷai ivvaḷavu tiruttamāṉa muṟaiyil ittuṇai aḻakaḻakāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷil tamiḻulakam eṅṅaṉam peṟṟup payaṉaṭaiya iyaṉṟirukkum eṉa kallūri, kumarakuruparar kuḻaintaippaḷḷi, nīlāmpikaiyār paltuṟaip payiṟcip paḷḷi, kā.cu. piḷḷai ārāycci maṉṟam iṉṉapala aṟappaṇi amaippukaḷai niṟuvip pēṇi vaḷarttalāl, it tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakam peṟṟuvarum nalaṅkaḷaic collavum kūṭumō?
cīriya kūṭṭuṟavu niṟuvaṉattiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, cevviya itaḻukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, kuṟaivilā niṟaivām patippukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, puttakak kaṭṭaṭa eḻilukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, vīṭṭu nūlaka vaippukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā, tokupput tiṟaṉukku eṭuttukkāṭṭā - kaḻakam eṉṉum orē viṭaitāṉē ivaṟṟukku uṇṭu!
taṉittamiḻ malaiyām maṟaimalaiyār toṇṭu viḷakkamuṟa, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar tam centamiḻc coṟpiṟap pākkappaṇi tulakkamuṟa, pēraṟiñar mu va viṉ tirukkuṟaḷurai vīṭellām oḷiceyya vāytta paṇikkaḷari kaḻakamē yaṉṟō!
ik kaḻakam tōṉṟiṟ ṟillaiyēl eṅkum tamiḻ etilum tamiḻ eṉṉum muḻakkam eḻuntirukkumō? ulakat tamiḻp palkalaik kaḻakamum, ulakat tamiḻc caṅkamum, ulakat tamiḻ ārāycci niṟuvaṉamum iṉṉapiṟa pēramaippu kaḷum tōṉṟutaṟku vaḻivakuttut tanta vaḷamaṉai kaḻakam eṉpatai eṇṇātirukka iyalumā?
kaḻakan tōṉṟiyatāl tōṉṟiya payaṉkaḷ ivaiyeṉiṉ, ak kaḻakattiṉ niṟuvaṉar ataṉ amaiccar, kaḻakan tōṉṟiya nāḷ toṭṭu innāḷvarai ataṉ ovvoru ceyal tiṟattilum oṉṟi uṭaṉāki iyakkivarum kaḻaka āṭciyāḷar tāmaraic celvar va. cuppaiyā piḷḷaiyiṉ uṭaṉpiṟappāy, vaḻikāṭṭiyāy, kuruvāy amaintavar ākiya tiruvaraṅkaṉār tōṉṟiya payaṉē mūlamum, muḷaivum viḷaivumāy amaintaṉa eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. ākaliṉ avartam varalāṟṟai aṟital, tamiḻaka maṟumalarcci varalāṟṟai aṟitalām eṉṉum kuṟippuṭaṉ aṟivōm.


2. teyvat tiruvaṭivam
kaṟcilai eṉat takunta kaṭṭuṭal; caṟṟu vaḻukkaiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya kaṉatta periya talai; nīṟuviḷaṅkum paranta neṟṟi; periya kaṇkaḷ; iṉikka iṉikkap pēcum kuṟunakai mukam; akaṉṟa mārpu, veḷḷiya uṭcaṭṭai; caṭṭaippaikaḷ yāvaṟṟilum pala vakaiyāṉa kaṭitak kaṭṭukaḷuṭaiya pottāṉ pōṭāta mēṟcaṭṭai; iṭuppiṟ cuṟṟiyuḷḷa cempaṭṭu; kaṇukkālukku mēṟpaṭṭa veḷḷai vēṭṭi; aintaṭi uyaramuḷḷa caṟṟuk kuṟainta uyaram; nimirntu etirnōkki naṭakkum vīranaṭai - ivai, tamiḻt tiruvaraṅkariṉ tōṟṟameṉa avartam maittuṉar maṟai tirunāvukkaracu kaṇṭatu kaṇṭavāṟu uraikkum kaviṉurai.
tōṟṟattāl ciṟantu viḷaṅkiya tiruvaraṅkar, tamakkut tamaiyaṉārāka amaintu vaḻikāṭṭiya ēṟṟattait, tāmaraic celvar va. cu. tam pavaḷa viḻāvil niṉaivu kūrntār :
eṉṉai inta nilaikku āḷākkiviṭṭavar eṉ tamaiyaṉār avarkaḷē. avarkaḷ paḻaiya itaḻkaḷait tokuttuk koṇṭē iruppārkaḷ. paḻaiya nūlkaḷait tēṭit tokuttuk koṇṭē iruppārkaḷ. appaṭiyellām avarkaḷ eṉakku vaḻikāṭṭiyataip piṉpaṟṟittāṉ nāṉ appaṭiyē ceytuvarukiṟēṉ. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ toṭarpiṉālē intat taṉittamiḻt toṇṭu ceyyum vāyppu eṉakku ēṟpaṭṭatu. ātalāl, eṉakku kiṭaikkappeṟum pukaḻukkum - perumaikkum uriyavarkaḷ eṉ arumait tamaiyaṉār tiruvaraṅkaṉār avarkaḷum, taṉittamiḻt tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭi kaḷārumē āvar (centamiḻc celvi cilampu 47, pakkam 744)
eṉpatu tam tamaiyaṉārai niṉaivukūrnta tampiyār urai.
intiya varalāṟu teṉṉakattil toṭaṅkiyē eḻutap peṟavēṇṭum eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷarkaḷiṉ muntai muṭivu. iṉṟai aṟiñarkaḷō, ulaka varalāṟē teṉṉakattil iruntu toṭaṅkiyē eḻutappeṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatai valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉar. ulaka mutal tāymoḻi, tamiḻē eṉpataiyum, māntaṉ mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya iṭam, kumarik kaṇṭamē eṉpataiyum teḷivupaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. ittaku pukaḻvāyntatu teṉṉakam.
teṉṉakattilum teṟkē cellac cellat tamiḻiṉ cīrmai ciṟantu viḷaṅkutal kaṇkūṭu. iṉṟum paḻantamiḻ vaḻakkāṟukaḷ paḻutupaṭṭuviṭāmal pōṟṟik kākkum vaḷamaiyōṭu ilaṅkuvatu paḻampukaḻp pāṇṭināṭēyām. pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ ciṟappāka, mupporuḷkaḷ ōtappeṟu kiṉṟaṉa. avai muttu, teṉṟal, tamiḻ eṉpaṉa.
tiruviḷaiyāṭal purāṇam pāṭiya parañcōtimuṉivar iṟaivaṉ teṟku nōkki naṭampurivatu ēṉ? eṉa oru viṉāvai eḻuppik koṇṭu, viṭaiyiṟukkiṟār: iṟaivaṉ taṉ āṭalāl viḷaiyum iḷaippait teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟu taṉ mukattil viciṟutalāl nīkkikkoḷvataṟkum, tirucceviyāl teṉṉaṉ tamiḻamiḻtam parukik kaḷaiyāṟutaṟkum teṟkunōkki naṭampurikiṉṟāṉām! ittaku teṉṉāṭṭiṉ naṉṉāṭām tirunelvēli cārnta pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyil piṟantār tiruvaraṅkaṉār.
pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyil muttucāmippiḷḷai eṉpār pērum perumaiyum viḷaṅkat tikaḻntār. avartam arumait tirumakaṉār vayiramuttuppiḷḷai. peṟṟōr tam makkaḷaik kaṇṇē maṇiyē vayiramē muttē eṉṟu pārāṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. pāḷai muttō, tam makaṉai vayiramuttākap peyariṭṭuc cīrōṭum ciṟappōṭum vaḷarttār. akkāla muṟaippaṭi uriya vayatil toṭakkak kalvi kaṟpittut tam maraput toḻilil payiṟṟiṉār. innilaiyil uriya tirumaṇa paruvam cārntār vayiramuttu.
pāṭu pukaḻ peṟṟa koṟkaip paḻanakaril aiyaṉpiḷḷai eṉpār oruvar iruntār. avartam arumait tuṇaiviyār pēcciyammaiyār. ivarkaḷ celvak kuḻantaiyāka 1868il ōr arumai makavu piṟantatu. am makaviṉ puṟa aḻakum aka aḻakum karuti vaittāṟ pōlac cuntarattammai eṉap peyarcūṭṭi vaḷarttaṉar! peyar viḷaṅkavanta avvaḻakammai, aḻakuruvām maṇapparuvam cārntār. avarē vayiramuttāṉ aḻakukku aḻaku ceyyum vāḻkkait tuṇaiyāka amaintār. vayira muttoṭu aḻakucārnta arumai, iyaṟkaiyoṭu poruntiya iṉimai vāyntatēyām. avvāṇṭu 1884. 
vayiramuttu cuntarattammai iṇainta iṉiya vāḻvu ettakaiyatu! poṉṉum maṇiyum - muttum - pavaḷamum - vayiramum oṉṟupaṭṭu viḷaṅkum aṇimālai taṇiyā aḻakiṉataṉṟō! avvaḻaku ceytatu ivarkaḷ vāḻvu! kuṟiñcip pūvil tēṉai eṭuttuc cantaṉa marattil cērttu vaikkappeṟṟa tēṉiṉ cuvaikkum naṟumaṇattiṟkum oppuṇṭō? appaṭi oppaṟat tikaḻntatu ivarkaḷ vāḻvu! ivarkaḷ iṉiya ilvāḻviṉ payaṉākak kiḷarnta makkaḷ aivar. avarkaḷ muttucāmi, tiruvaraṅkam, aiyamperumāḷ, cuppaiyā, kuppammāḷ eṉpār. tiruvaraṅkar 1890ām āṇṭu mēttiṅkaḷil piṟantār.
kātal iruvar karuttu orumittu naṭāttiya illaṟam, patiṉaintu āṇṭukaḷkūṭa muṟṟa muṭiya naṭantēṟiṟṟillai. ataṟkuḷ 1899il vayiramuttar iṟaiyaṭi eytiṉār. ciṉṉañ ciṟu makkaḷaik koṇṭa muppatu vayatē nirampiya cuntarattammai eṉṉa pāṭupaṭṭiruppār! pañcupaṭāp pāṭupaṭṭu, makkaḷai nōkki maṉavuṟutiyāl vāḻnta avar, tantaiyumākit tam kaṭaṉ āṟṟiṉār. tāymaiyiṉ perumaiyē perumai! veṟukkattakka vāḻvilē, urukkam talaippaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu! kuṭumpap perukkamē kuṟiyāyp, peṇmai peruvāḻvu vāḻac cūḻkiṉṟatu! itu paṭaippiṉ arumaippāṭēyām!
iḷamaṉaivikkum iḷamakkaḷukkum tantaiyār vaippu niti vaittuc ceṉṟārā? nilapulaṉkaḷ nirampa viṭṭuc ceṉṟārā? kaṉṟukālikaḷ kaṇakkiṟantu iruntaṉavā? illai! illai; vaṟumaiyoṉṟē vaippup poruḷāy viṭṭuc ceṉṟār! vaḻi vaḻi vanta vīṭu oṉṟumē uṭaimaiyāy iruntatu!
toṇṭāṟ paḻutta tamiḻk kiḻavar cuppaiyā piḷḷai avarkaḷ, tam tantaiyāraippaṟṟit tam aṉṉaiyār vaḻiyāka aṟinta ceytikaḷai niṉaivu kūrntu cuṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar :
eṉ arumait tantaiyār avarkaḷ tiruvuruvam eṉ maṉak kaṇṇil teḷivākap paṭavillai. avarkaḷ tōṟṟam perumpāṉmai eṉ arumai aṇṇaṉār va. tiruvaraṅkaṉār pōla irukkumām. tirumaṇam ākumpōtu avarkaṭku 27 akavai irukkum eṉat terikiṉṟatu. appōtu eṉ arumai aṉṉaiyārkku akavai 16. avarkaḷ kaṭṭuttiṭṭamāṉa uṭal amaippuṭaiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum yāvariṭamum aṉpāyp pēci naṭpuk koḷḷum taṉmai uṭaiyavarkaḷ eṉṟum, uṟaviṉar kaḷiṭamum aṉpu kāṭṭi varupavarkaḷ eṉṟum avarkaḷ viḻi kavarcci taruvatāy irukkum eṉṟum aṉṉaiyār terivippa tuṇṭu.
palvakaip poruḷkaḷ viṟkum kaṭai (ṣāp)yil kaṇakku vēlaipārttu vantārkaḷām. tirunelvēlikkuc ceṉṟu pa.ta, eṉṟa mukavariyiluḷḷa tulukkar kaṭaiyil poruḷkaḷai vāṅkuvataṟkuc celvārkaḷām. appaṭip pōy varukaiyil orunāḷ aiyā, vayiramuttupiḷḷai! nīr contamāka oru kaṭai toṭaṅkum. umakku vēṇṭiya poruḷ kaḷaik kaṭaṉākat tarukiṟēṉ eṉṟu collavē taṉikkaṭai tiṟantu naṭatti vantārkaḷām. appaṭi naṭatti varumpōtu mottamāka āmaṇakku muttu, maṇṇeṇ ṇeyt takarat tūkku (ṭiṉ) ākiyavai vāṅki vīṭṭil vaittum, viyāḻakkiḻamai tōṟum cantaiyil vaittum viṟka ēṟpāṭu ceytārkaḷām. inta muṟaiyil toḻil vaḷarntōṅki varukaiyil tiṭumeṉak kāyccalum neñcu valiyum ēṟpaṭṭu mūṉṟu nāḷ varai tuṉpuṟṟu iṟaiyaṭi cērntārkaḷām. appōtu eṉ mūtta tamaiyaṉār muttucāmi piḷḷaikkuk kaṭaiyai naṭattuvataṟkup paḻakkam pōtātu. ciṟu piḷḷait taṉam. uṟaviṉar evarum utavi ceyya muṉvaravillai. eṉavē, mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷil kaṭaiyai niṟutti atiluḷḷa miccap poruḷkaḷai uṟaviṉar maḷikaik kaṭai oṉṟil vaittuviṭṭu antak kaṭaiyil paṇi ēṟṟukkoṇṭār.
koṭitu koṭitu vaṟumai koṭitu
	ataṉiṉum koṭitu iḷamaiyil vaṟumai
eṉṟār auvaiyār. ataṉiṉum koṭitu, iḷaṅkaim maiyāyk, kuṟukuṟu naṭantu ciṟukai nīṭṭum iḷamakkaḷaip pēṇik kākkum koṭu vaṟumaiyām!
ak koṭu vaṟumaiyiṉ iṭaiyē aṉṟō, āliṉ aṭimaram cāyavum vīḻtu marattait tāṅkik kāppatu pōlac cuntarattammaiyār kuṭumpattaik kāttār! avar kātta kāval kuṭumpak kāval oṉṟu maṭṭumā? avaṉ aṉṟu kātta kāvaliṉ ūṭṭamum tiṟamum uḻaippum allavō, aruntamiḻ aṉṉaikkup paṉṉūṟu aṇikalaṅkaḷaip pūṭṭip pūṭṭip pukaḻuṟuttum naṉmakkaḷ iruvarait tantatu! anta vayiramaṇic celvaṅkaḷai vayiṟṟakattut tāṅki vaḷarttut toṇṭukku āḷākkit, toṇṭar cīr paravuvār pōlat tam makkaḷ toṇṭilē tiḷaittu niṉṟa tūya teyvat tāymai vāḻvu atuvēyaṉṟō! ākaliṉ, iṭumpaikku iṭumpai tantu, iṉmaikku iṉmai ceytu iṇaiyaṟṟu vāḻnta avvarumai aṉṉaiyār vāḻvu uṉṉuntōṟum urukkamum atē poḻutil uramum ūṭṭuvatāyt tikaḻkiṉṟatām!


3. vāḻum vaḻikāṭṭi
kuṭumpa nilai eppaṭi? muppattōrāṇṭuk kaimmai nōṉput tāy! patiṉmūṉṟāṇṭu muttucāmi! pattāṇṭut tiruvaraṅkar! ēḻāṇṭu aiyam perumāḷ! mūṉṟāṇṭuc cuppaiyā! ōrāṇṭum nirampāta kuppammāḷ! varuvāy? varu vāy iruntāltāṉē varuvāy uṇṭu? poruḷ varum vāyē aṭaipaṭṭuviṭṭatē!
muttucāmi tantaiyār vaittucceṉṟa kaṭaiyai mūṭi uṟaviṉar kaṭaiyil paṇiyil amarntār! tiruvaraṅkar kalvik kātal taṭaiyuṟṟatu. paṭippai iraṇṭām paṭivattuṭaṉ muṭittu mūṉṟām paṭivam cellum aḷavilē paṭippai viṭuttu vēlai tēṭiṉār. anta iḷam paruvam vēlaik kuriyatō? kalvikkuriya vaḷamaiyāṉa iḷamai, vaṟumaiyāl vēlaikkut taḷḷuṇṭu ceṉṟatu. vaḻakkaṟiñar oruvariṉ eḻuttarāka ēviṟṟu. av ēvutal viruppantaravillai. viraivil ataṉai viṭuttu vēṟu vēlaikku kiḷappiṟṟu! vēlai kaikkuḷḷēyō kiṭaikkiṟatu? pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaiyai viṭuttu tūttukkuṭikkuc ceṉṟār iḷaiya araṅkaṉār. tuṭippum tuṇivum kūṭiviṭumāṉāl iḷamaikku īṭu uṇṭō? ciṟṟāḷ vēlai eṭṭāḷ vēlai eṉṉum paḻamoḻi meyyeṉpatai aṟiyār evar?
kālpōṉa pōkkilē pōykkoṇṭiruntālum ākūḻ oṉṟu amaintuviṭumpōtu kaiyil kaṉap poruḷ cikkiviṭum pōlum! kuṟumpūḻ vēṭṭuvaṉ vaṟuṅkaiyum varumē; yāṉai vēṭṭuvaṉ yāṉaiyum peṟumē eṉṉum kōpperuñcōḻaṉ poruṇmoḻik kāñci (puṟam. 214) meyyātaṟkup pōlum, araṅkaṉār vēlai tēṭit tūttukkuṭikkuc ceṉṟamai!
tūttukkuṭiyil vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntaram piḷḷai eṉpār oruvar iruntār. avar civaneṟic celvar; cemporuḷ tuṇiviṉar; tūttukkuṭi caivacittānta capaiyiṉ toṭarpu uṭaiyavar. āciriyar maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ
māṭṭup pēraṉpu kaṉinta peruntiruvāḷar. avartam uṟavum uḻuvalaṉpum iḷaiya araṅkarkku vāyttatu, vāṉpār payirkku vaḷamaḻai poḻintatu pōlāyiṟṟu! tikkut teriyāta kāṭṭil terumantu alamaruvārkkut ticai terintu vaḻikāṭṭum tiṟalāḷi uṟavu vāyttamai pōl āyiṟṟu! ammaṭṭō, vāḻat tuṭikkum vaḻitēṭuvārkku vaḻikūṭṭum vaḷḷaṉmaiyum vāyppatupōl āyiṟṟu! tūttukkuṭiyil nērnta tiruppamē araṅkaṉārait tamiḻ araṅkarāyc ceytatu eṉpatu veḷippaṭai.
tūttukkuṭi vaṇika nilaiyamoṉṟil paṇi ceytār tiruvaraṅkar. vaṇikap payiṟci peṟutaṟku vāyppum, varuṅkāla vāḻvukku aṭittaḷamumākat tūttukkuṭi vāḻvu amaintatē aṉṟi, maṉaniṟaivāṉa paṇiyākavō, tam kuṭumpattiṉ muṭṭaṟukka utavum varuvāyāṉa paṇiyākavō vaṇika nilaiyap paṇi amaiyavillai. in nilaiyil āṅkiruttal kuṭi ceyval eṉṉum oruvaṟku ākāmaiyai uṇarntār araṅkar. vīṭṭār icaivu kēṭṭuc celvatum iyalātu eṉa uṇarntār. ataṉāl vaḻutūr aḻakiya cuntaraṉār vaḻikāṭṭaliṉpaṭi, tūttukkuṭiyil iruntu kappal ēṟik koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār.
koḻumpu cērnta piṉṉarē tam arumai aṉṉaiyārkkum, uṭaṉpiṟantārkkum ceyti viṭuttār. araṅkariṉ pirivu kuṭumpattai alaittatu. eṉiṉum avartam ārva muyaṟcip perukkum urukkiyatu! kuṭumpap poṟaiyum, ataṉ nalamum karuti irupālum niṟaivu koḷḷum nilaimai uruvākiyatu. itu nikaḻntatu 1907ām āṇṭilākum. appoḻutu araṅkaṉār akavai 17.
koḻumpu mānakar cērnta araṅkar, tirunelvēliyaic cārnta * cicuva. eṉṉum vaṇika niṟuvaṉattil paṇi ēṟṟār. paṇi eṉṟāl eppaṭi? kaṭaikkaṇakku eḻutum eḻuttar paṇi! eḻuttāḷar eḻuttukaḷai ellām muttiraiyiṭap piṟanta perumakaṉār, kaṭaikkakku eḻutum paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭār. appaṇikku, aṇi cērkka amaittār. ūḻaiyum vellum uram; uyarnta kuṟikkōḷ; kuṭumpattaik kākkum puravāṇmai; tammaic cāṉṟōrākkum takavu; tammaic cārntārāl talaimaiyākak koḷḷappeṟum pīṭu; tamiḻk kalvi ūṟṟam; camayac cālpum cikkeṉap piṭikkum kaṭaippiṭiyum; neñcārnta nērmai; vañcamaṟṟa uḻaippu; ulakattōṭu oṭṭa oḻukum oppuravu; tēṉeṉa iṉiya kūṟit tiḷaikkum nānayam; kālan tavaṟāk karutukōḷ; cuṟṟan taḻūum urimaippāṭu; talaimaiyai matittoḻukum taṉipperun takavu; putiyaṉa kāṇṭalil peru vēṭkai; aṟivārāycciyil ellaiyillā ārvap perukku-ivvaḷavu arum peruṅkuṇa nalaṅkaḷum iḷamaiyilēyē vāynta peruntiruviṉar eppaṇiyil pukuntāl eṉṉa? enta iṭattil entac cūḻalil iruntāl eṉṉa? kālamum iṭamum cūḻalum kaikaṭṭi niṉṟu maṭi taṟṟut tuṇai ceytalukku aiyamuṇṭō?
tiruvaraṅkaṉār īḻattil irunta kālaiyil uḻaitta uḻaippai uṭaṉiruntu kaṇṭavar, piṉṉāḷil maturait tiruñāṉa campantar tirumaṭat talaivarākat tikaḻnta 
tiru. cōmacuntara ñāṉacampanta paramāccāriyār avarkaḷāvar. avarkaḷ araṅkaraip paṟṟiya niṉaiviṉai oru muṟaikku irumuṟai niṉaivu kūrntu eḻutiṉār :
nam pēraṉpar tiruvaraṅkampiḷḷai avarkaḷ, eṭutta kāriyattai muṟṟuppeṟac ceyvatil caliyā uḻaippum, taḷarā ūkkamum, ayarā ārvamum, añcā neñcamum, tamiḻppaṟṟum iṟaivaṉiṭattil māṟāta aṉpum koṇṭavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ nāḷ oṉṟukku mūṉṟu maṇi nēram maṭṭum tūṅki, pākki 21 maṇi nēramum uḻaittu vanta kālamum uṇṭu eṉṟu kaḻakattil 1008vatu veḷiyīṭṭu viḻā malarukku eḻutiya vāḻtturaiyil terivikkiṟār. ataṉ mēlum viḷakkamākak kaḻakap poṉ viḻā vāḻttil terivittuḷḷār maturai aṭikaḷār.
ik kaḻakattiṟkup paṅkukaḷ cērttu varaiyaṟukkappeṟṟa niṟuvaṉamāka uruvākka ataṟkeṉṟē avataritta namatu matippiṟkuriya tiruvāḷar va. tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷai avarkaḷ evvaḷavu pāṭupāṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatai nām naṉkaṟivōm. avarkaḷ ilaṅkaiyil irunta kālattil iravu mūṉṟu maṇi nēram maṭṭumē tūṅkuvatum maṟṟa 21 maṇi nēramum ayarātu uḻaittu vantatum nām nēril kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟirukkiṟōm. aḷavukku miñciṉa uḻaippāl piṉṉar avarkaḷ uṭal ciṟitu nalivuṟṟatum uṇṭu. āṉāl,
ceyaṟkariya ceyvār periyar ciṟiyar,
	ceyaṟkariya ceykalā tār
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷaiyum,
tuṉpam uṟavaṉiṉum ceyka tuṇivāṟṟi,
	iṉpam payakkum viṉai
eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷaiyum avarkaḷ naṭaimuṟaiyil kaiyāṇṭu vantataṉ kāraṇattiṉālētāṉ intak kaḻakattai veṟṟi
karamāka avarkaḷ naṭattivara muṭintatu.
tiruvaraṅkariṉ vaḷamāṉa iḷaneñcil aḻakiya cuntaram piḷḷai, maṟaimalai eṉṉum vittiṉai ūṉṟiṉār allarō! avvittu muḷaittu ilaiyoṭu veḷippaṭum vāyppum aṭuttē nikaḻntatu. aṭikaḷār eḻutiya nūlkaḷait tiruvaraṅkar taruvittār. avaṟṟaip parukuvaṉ aṉṉa ārvattarāyp payiṉṟār; payiṉṟa ceytikaḷai vāḷā oḻiya viṭāmal tammottavarkkum, uyarntavarkkum payiṟṟiṉār. iyalpilēyē, kātalākik kacintu kaṇṇīr malki ōtuvār tammai naṉṉeṟik kuyppatu, vētam nāṉkiṉum meyp poruḷāvatu, nātaṉ nāmam namacciyāyavē eṉṟu kaṇṭavar araṅkar. ataṉai aṭaivataṟkuk kurumūrttamāy eḻuntaruḷi iruppavar tavattiru maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷārē eṉṉum teḷivukku vantār! ātalāl, tam neñcat tāmarai iruppil aṭikaḷārai irutti vaittu vaḻipaṭṭār. kāṇāmal kaṇṭaṭainta kurumaṇiyaik kaṇṇuṟak kaṇṭukaḷippuṟum kātal perukiṟṟu. itaṉaip perukkiyatil mīṇṭum tūttuk kuṭikkum, aḻakiya cuntaraṉārkkum perum paṅku uṇṭu.
1910ām āṇṭu mutal tavattiru maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷār cila āṇṭukaḷ toṭarntu tūttukkuṭi caivacittānta capaiyiṉ āṇṭu viḻāt talaimai pūṇṭār; talaimaikkaṇ, arumperum poḻivukaḷ ceytār; piṟar poḻivu toṭarpākavum niṟaivurai yāṟṟiṉār. aṭikaḷār varukai mutal viṭaipeṟal īṟāka nikaḻnta nikaḻccikaḷaiyum, vaḻaṅkiya amuta moḻi kaḷaiyum tūttukkuṭi aṉparkaḷ vaḻiyākavum, kuṟippāka aḻakiya cuntarar vaḻiyākavum araṅkar aṟintār. aṭikkaṭi avaṟṟaik kēḷviyuṟṟu, eṉṟu kolō kaṇkuḷirak kāṇu nāḷē eṉṟu ēkkuṟṟār. in nilaiyil aṭikaḷāraik koḻumpukku aḻaittup payaṉ koḷḷum oru muṭivukku vantār.
tiruvaraṅkar, uṇmait tamiḻar, uḻuvalaṉpar, urimait toṭarpar, vaṇikap perumakkaḷ, vaḷḷaṉmaic celvar, paṭṭam patavi vīṟuṭaiya tamiḻ aṟiñar ākiyavarkaḷōṭu aṇukkat toṭarpu koṇṭu aṭikaḷāraik koḻumpukku aḻaittup pēṟu koḷḷum viḻuppattai eṭutturaittār. naṉmakkaḷ kūṭṭam nayantu pārāṭṭiyatu. araṅkaṉār ārvattiṟku araṇākip pulamait tuṇaiyum poruḷ tuṇaiyum puriyak kiḷarntatu. araṅkaṉār viḻaivu veṟṟik kaṉiyāka ilaṅkalāyiṟṟu.
pallāvarattil iruntu koḻumpuc celavu mēṟkoṇṭa aṭikaḷār, tūttukkuṭi caivacittānta capaiyiṉ āṇṭu viḻāvil talaimaiyēṟkum poṟuppum ēṟṟuk koṇṭiruntār. 20-12-1913, 21-12-1913 ākiya iraṇṭu nāḷkaḷum ariya poḻivukaḷ ceytār. 24-12-1913il tūttukkuṭi kīḻūr civañāṉap pirakāca capai viḻāvil, ceṉṉai kiruṭṭiṇavēṇi ammaiyār pulamait tiṟaṉaip pārāṭṭip paṇṭitai eṉṉum paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic ciṟappittār. 4-1-1914il tūttukkuṭiyil tiruñāṉa campantaraip paṟṟip poḻintār. 
6-1-1914 mālaiyil tūttukkuṭiyil iruntu kappalēṟik koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār. 7-1-1914 kālaiyil koḻumput tuṟaiyai aṭaintār aṭikaḷ.
* cicuva. eṉpatu cāttāṉ kuḷam civakāminātapiḷḷai, ammaṉpuram cupparāya piḷḷai, kulacēkara paṭṭiṉam vayiravanātapiḷḷai eṉṉum mūvarum naṭāttiya kūṭṭu niṟuvaṉac curukkap peyarākum.



4. īḻattil tamiḻmalai
aṭikaḷ vantucērum kappalai aṟintu koṇṭirunta araṅkar, tam aṉparkaḷum aṇukkarkaḷum nakarapperu
makkaḷum tiraṇṭirukka varavēṟṟu makiḻntār. ataṟku muṉṉar aṉpuk kaṭitaṅkaḷ vāyilāka maṭṭum aṟintirunta araṅkariṉ iḷamaip polivaiyum, vaḷamaiyuḷattaiyum nayattaku paṇpu nalaṅkaḷaiyum nēril kaṇṭaṟinta aṭikaḷār maṭṭaṟṟa makiḻvu koṇṭār. tammai īḻattiṟku iv viḷaiñartāmō aḻaittār? ivartam ārvamum āṟṟalumtām eṉṉē! eṉṉē! iv viḷaiñarpāl iṅkuṟaiyum poruṭ celvarkaḷum aruṭ celvarkaḷum koṇṭuḷḷa īṭupāṭu
tām eṉṉē! eṉṉē! emakkut toṇṭu ceyya veṉṟē iṟaivaṉ iv varaṅka vaḷḷalai viṭuttaruḷiṉāṉ kollō! eṉṟu palavāka eṇṇi uḷantatumpiṉār; uvakai kūrntār!
tavattiru aṭikaḷār camayap pulamaiyaiyum tamiḻ vīṟukōḷaiyum nūlkaḷ vaḻiyākavum, avartam poḻivukaḷaik kēṭṭuc cuvaitta ārvalar vaḻiyākavumē aṟintirunta araṅkar, aṭikaḷāriṉ tōṟṟap polivilum tammai maṟantu mayaṅki oṉṟiṉār! civakkōlam eṉpatum itutāṉā! centaṇmaiyum antaṇmai eṉpatum itutāṉā! eṉṉai vaḻivaḻiyāka āṭkoṇṭaruḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟa pēruḷḷap perukkākiya tiruvaruṭ kōlam eṉpatum itutāṉō! vaṇaṅki vaḻipaṭattakka iṟai mūrttam eḷiyēṉ poruṭṭāka oruvaṭivu koṇṭu eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum pēṟē pēṟu! eṉṟu tīrāk kātalarāy uḷḷam aḷḷūṟi ūṟṟeṭukka niṉṟār.
varavēṟpu aṟimukam eṉpaṉa vellām muṟaiyē nikaḻntaṉa. aṭikaḷārkku vaḷamelām oruṅku amainta vaḷamaṉai oṉṟil taṅkum vāyppuc ceyyap peṟṟiruntatu. mutaṟ kūṭṭam 10-1-14ām nāḷ mālaiyil koḻumpu tampaiyā cattirattil nikaḻntatu. toṭakka nikaḻcci evvāṟu amaintatu?
peruṅkūṭṭamākat tiraṇṭatu. uṇmaiyāṉa camaya aṉparkaḷ kūṭṭam atu. koḻumpu nakaril perumai poruntiya mutaliyār kailāca piḷḷai, aṭikaḷaip paṟṟiya arumai perumaikaḷai avaiyōrkku eṭuttukkūṟi varavēṟṟār.
poṉmēṉiyil kāviyāṭai oḷirum tōṟṟam, mukkāṭiṭṭa tāmarai pōṉṟa tirumukap polivu, iṉimaiyē uruveṭuttālaṉaiya cāyal, āya eḻil vaṭivām aṭikaḷiṉ tirumukattai nōkkiyapaṭiyē avaiyōr acaiyāmal viḻitta imai mūṭā yōkiyar āṉār. eṅkum malaikkāṭṭiṉ amaiti. aṭikaḷ puṉitavāy malarntu, kaṇmūṭik, kai kuvittu maṉamuruka-nīrvāra-kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu, ñāṉacampantar vāḻttukaḷaip pāṭi muṭittār.
aṭikaḷiṉ icai iṉimai, iṉimaikku iṟaivaṉākum. avviṉṉōcai iṉpa veḷḷattil paṭinta avaiyōr tammaiyum ulakaiyum uṇarntilar. ituvarai kaṇṭum kēṭṭu mirāta cuvaittirāta iṉpa nilaiyaṭaintaṉar. avaiyōr uṇarcci pēcupavaraiyum paṟṟikkoḷḷum allavā? pēriṉpa ulakattuk kuyil aṉparkaḷē eṉṟu pēcat toṭaṅkiyatu. avvaḷavutāṉ! avaiyōr tēṉuṇṭa vaṇṭeṉa mēlum iṉpa mayakkattil kalaimakaḷ viḻāp patumaikaḷāyc camaintaṉar. ellōruṭaiya aṟivum aṭikaḷ aṟivil oṉṟiviṭṭatu. kaṟpaṉai kaṭanta aṭikaḷiṉ coṟpoḻiviṉ patippil tiḷaikkalāyiṟṟu.
pēcat toṭaṅkiya tamiḻp perumāṉ- civaperumāṉ - etupaṟṟip pēciyiruppār? karuta vēṇṭiyatillai. tiruñāṉacampantaraip paṟṟittāṉ. aṭikaḷ tam āruyiriṉum ciṟanta appiḷḷaip perumāṉaippaṟṟip pēci muṭittaṉar. mutal veṟṟi. muḻu veṟṟi. veṟṟi, veṟṟi peṟa vēṇṭu
meṉṟu tavaṅkiṭantatu, ataṉ payaṉāy aṭikaḷ pēccil kalantu veṟṟi peṟṟateṉṟē colla vēṇṭum. iṉpamō tuṉpamō colliyaṉṟit tīrātu. aṭikaḷiṉ coṟpoḻi
viṉpattil makiḻntavarkaḷ am makiḻcciyait tammuḷ aṭakkum āṟṟalaṟṟavarāyiṉar. aṭikaḷ pēccu avvaḷavu uyarntatu. mūvulakattum kaṭavuḷiṭattuṅkūṭa avvaḷaviṉpam irukkātu. ākavē, maṟunāḷ aṭikaḷ irukkumiṭattai eṇṇaṟṟa aṉparkaḷ nāḷellām cūḻntukoṇṭaṉar. aṭikaḷum caḷaikkavillai. vantavarkaḷiṭam iṉpamuṟap pēciṉār. mēlum avarkaḷ iṉpuṟṟuc ceṉṟaṉar. (maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ varalāṟu 205 - 6)
7-1-1914il koḻumpukku vantu cērntu aṭikaḷ 24-3-1914 varai āṅkiruntār. iv viraṇṭarait tiṅkaḷ kālamum tiruvaraṅkar aṭikaḷukku aṉparāy, aṇukkarāy, aṟivaṟinta ārvalarāy, iv vellāvaṟṟaiyum kaṭanta toṇṭarāyt tikaḻntār. ettaṉai iṭaṅkaḷil kūṭṭam; ettaṉai perumakkaḷ toṭarpu; evvaḷavu poruḷ taṇṭal; ellāvaṟṟilum tiruvaraṅkar tūya uḷḷam paṭintu paṭintu toṇṭāṟṟiyatu.
īḻattiṟku ceṉṟa aṭikaḷ paṉṉiru kūṭṭaṅkaḷil poḻivu ceytār; tiruvaruṭ celvattaiyum, centamiḻc celvattaiyum vāri vāri vaḻaṅkiṉār! vaḻaṅkiya aṭikaḷukkup poruṭ celvam vāri vaḻaṅkum kaṭappāṭṭil muṉaintu niṉṟār tiruvaraṅkar. aṭikaḷ pallāvarattil tirumāḷikai oṉṟu eḻupputalil ūṉṟiyiruntār. ataṟku vēṇṭum poruṭkuṟaiyāl iṭaruṟṟiruntār. ak kuṟaiyait tīrkkattakka vakaiyil aṭikaḷārkku araṅkar poruḷīṭṭi utaviṉār. aṭikaḷāriṉ mutaṟ cuṟṟulāvilēyē rū. 1888 kiṭaikka vakai ceytār tiruvaraṅkar. ammaṭṭō, aṭikaḷāriṉ aṟivākkap paṇikaḷukku utavum vakaiyil tiṅkaḷ tōṟum koḻumpuc celvarkaḷ vaḻiyākat tokai kiṭaikkavum vakai ceytār. aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷait taruvittu viṟṟut tantum utaviṉār. piṉṉē, aṭikaḷār accakam oṉṟu niṟuvutaṟku muṉaintār. ataṟkum araṅkar perumporuḷ tiraṭṭit tantār. aṭikaḷār tam aṟivuk kaṭal (ñāṉacākaram) itaḻil tiruvaraṅkar toṇṭiṉai eḻutip pārāṭṭiṉār :
namatu accuk kūṭattiṟkāka aṉparkaḷiṭam poruḷ tiraṭṭit tara muṉvantu niṉṟu, ceṉṟa oṉṟaraiyāṇṭukaḷāka iṭaiyaṟātu uḻaittu utavi ceytuvarum nam aṉpuruvāṉa srīmāṉ va. tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷaiyarkaḷiṉ pērupakārac ceykaikkut tiruvaḷḷuva nāyaṉār,
ceyyāmaṟ ceyta utavikku vaiyakamum,
	vāṉakamum āṟṟal aritu
eṉṟaruḷic ceytavaṇṇam evvakaiyāṉa kaimmāṟutāṉ ceyyak kūṭum? nammarumaic centamiḻ moḻiyum caiva cintānta ñāṉamum paravumporuṭṭu nāmeṭuttuvarum naṉmuyaṟcikaḷ taḷarāmal naṭaipeṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟu eṇṇiya avarkaḷiṉ puṇṇiya niṉaivum, an niṉaivai nilaipeṟac ceyta avarkaḷiṉ ariya periya muyaṟciyum ellām valla iṟaivaṉaruḷai iṭaiyaṟātu iḻukkavallaṉa. avarkaḷ attiruvaruṭ pēṟṟāl varum ellāc celvaṅkaḷaiyum peṟṟu emṉōrkku uṟutuṇaiyāy iṉitu vāḻvārkaḷāka eṉṟu vāḻttukiṉṟōm. iṉi srīmāṉ tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷaiyiṉ vēṇṭukōṭ kiṇaṅkip puṇṇiyat tiruvāḷarāṉa kulacēkaraṉpaṭṭiṉam srīmāṉ rā. pa. centilāṟumukam piḷḷaiyarkaḷ eḻunūṟṟaimpatu rūpāvum, tayāḷakuṇap pirapuvāṉa ku. pa. periyanāyakam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷ ainnūṟu rūpāvum namatu accukkūṭattiṟkeṉṟu tarumamāka utavi, uṭaṉē uyarnta putiya acciyantiram vāṅkumpaṭi muṉ muyaṟciyuṅ kāṭṭiya arumperun takaimai eḻumai eḻu piṟappum maṟakkaṟpālataṉṟu.
accakat tiṟappu viḻā aṟikkaiyilum araṅkarai aṭikaḷ niṉaivu kūrntār :
namakkē contamāka ōr accukkūṭam tiṟakka vēṇṭumeṉṟu pala āṇṭukaḷāka uḷḷattiṟ koṇṭirunta peruviruppattai ellā nalattiṟkum uṟaiviṭamāṉa nam āṇṭavaṉ tiruvaruḷum avvaruḷvaḻi niṟpavarāṉa aṉpar srīmāṉ va. tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷaiyavarkaḷum niṟaivēṟac ceyta pērutaviyiṉāl cālivākaṉacakam 1838 naḷa varuṭam kārttikai mātam 26n tēti (12 - 12 - 1916) putaṉ kiḻamai kālaiyil ēḻarai maṇikkuppiṉ eṭṭu maṇikkuḷḷākap pallāvaram namatu camaraca caṉmārkka nilaiyattiṉkaṇ namakkē contamāṉa ṭi.em eṉṉum peyar vāynta nam accukkūṭam nilaipeṟuttappaṭṭuc caiva maṅkaḷak kiriyaikaḷōṭu tiṟakkappaṭalāyiṟṟu.
accakat tiṟappu viḻā nikaḻnta aṉṟum, ataṟku utaviyām poruṭṭut, tiruvaraṅkar tām naṉkoṭaiyākat taṇṭiya rūpā aṟunūṟai viṭuttiruntār. ittakaiya arumaiyai uṇarntatāltāṉ, aṭikaṭku utavac civapirāṉāl aṉuppappaṭṭavar tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷaiyāvar eṉṟum (maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ varalāṟu 199) tiruvaraṅkar utaviyillā viṭṭāl aṭikaḷ kavalaiyiṉṟi iṉpamāy - ciṟappuṭaṉ tammaṟiviṉāl tamiḻakattiṉai uyarttirukka muṭiyumā? eṉṟum (mēṟpaṭi 250) nēril uṇarntavar pārāṭṭi makiḻntaṉar.
toṭarntu aṭikaḷārkku muṭṭuppāṭu iṉṟi utaviya tiruvaraṅkar aṭikaḷārai mīṇṭum koḻumpukku aḻaittār. 21-5-1917il koḻumpukkup puṟappaṭṭa aṭikaḷār 28-9-1917il mīṇṭār. ēṟattāḻa nāṉku tiṅkaḷāka amainta ic cuṟṟulāvil aṭikaḷiṉ pulamaic celvattai īḻam peṟṟup pēriṉpam tuykkavum, īḻap perumakkaḷiṉ naṉkoṭaic celvattāl aṭikaḷār tam tavapperum paṇikaḷai mēlum mēlum ciṟakkac ceyyavum vāyttatu. aṉṟiyum araṅkariṉ arumai iḷaval va. cuppaiyā avarkaḷum aṭikaḷāruṭaṉ aṇukkarāy aṉparāyt tikaḻavum vāyttatu.
ivviraṇṭāñ cuṟṟulāvil aṭikaḷārkku vēṇṭum nalaṅkaḷellām cūḻnta pēṇiyatuṭaṉ, naṉkoṭai taṇṭi rū. 1797um tiruvaraṅkar vaḻaṅkiṉār. avartam ārvat toṇṭil muḻutuṟa āḻnta aṭikaḷār oru peruṅ kūṭṭattil nīvir nāyaṉmār aṟupattu mūvarai aṟivīr; aṟupattunāṉkām nāyaṉār oruvaruḷār. avarē it tiruvaraṅkar eṉṟu pēraṉpu taḻaikkap pārāṭṭi makiḻntār. araṅkar, toṇṭarāy  aṭikaḷār toṇṭarcīr paravuvārāy amainta peṟṟi itu.
aṭikaḷārum araṅkarum aṭikkaṭi kaṭitat toṭarpu koṇṭaṉar. aṟivut toṇṭil talaippaṭṭa avarkaḷ uḷḷam ārvattāl untappeṟṟu aṉpup piṇaippil oṉṟiyatu. eṭṭā iṟaiyōṭum māntarai iṇaittu vaikkum aṉpupperukku evaraiyē iṇaikkātu? irupālum perukiya aṉpiṉ mukiḻppu, teyvat tirukkōlamāy uḷḷelām aḷḷūṟi niṉṟatu.
aṉpuruvāṉa celvac ciruñcīvi tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷai avarkaḷukkuc civaperumāṉ ṟiruvaruḷāl ellā nalaṅkaḷum uṇṭākuka. namatu accuk kūṭattiṟkup poruḷutavi ceyya icainta aṉparkaḷiṭam tāṅkaḷ aṉpu kūrntu tiraṭṭiyaṉupyiya rūpā nūṟum vantatu. piṟarkkeṉa vāḻum peruntakaimai vāynta taṅkaḷ pēruḷḷattil eḻuntaruḷi yirukkum nam civaperumāṉ taṅkaḷukku nalvāḻ nāḷaiyum kaḷaṅkamaṟṟa tūya utavi niṉaivukaḷaiyum mēṉmēl aruḷit taṅkaḷai ellā vakaiyilum mēmpaṭac ceyvāṉāka. tāṅkaḷ iṉṉuñ ciṟitu viṭātu muyaṉṟāl tokai viraiviṟṟiraḷum. nam kavalaikaḷellām akalum; ulakattiṟ ṟamiḻum caivamum nattāmaṇi viḷakkaṅkaḷāyt tikaḻum. taṅkaḷ pukaḻum puṇṇiyamuṅ kuṉṟiṉ mēliṭṭa viḷakkup pōl tikaḻum eṉṟu aṭikaḷār 29-6-1916il araṅkarukkuk kaṭitam varaintār. pārāṭṭum naṉṟiyum palapaṭa uraittār.
ilaṅkaikkuc ceṉṟu iraṇṭām cuṟṟulāvai muṭittuk koṇṭu pallāvaram mīṇṭa aṭikaḷār 6-8-1917il eṉatiru kaṇmaṇiyāy aṉpuruvāy viḷaṅkum celvac cīrañcīvi tiruvaraṅkam piḷḷaiyavarkaṭku nam perumāṉ tiruvaruḷāl ellā nalaṅkaḷum uṇṭākuka.
taṅkaḷ pēraṉpiṉ perukkālum nam miṟaivaṉ tiruvaruḷ vaḷattālum cukamāka iṅku vantu cērntēṉ. nam perumāṉ tiruvaruṭ pēṟṟaiyum caiva camaya viḷakkattaiyu mēyallāmal piṟitetaṉaiyum kaimmāṟāka nōkkāmal uṇmaiyaṉpōṭu uḻaittu varum taṅkaḷ arumperuñ ceyal ulakiṉkaṇ maṟṟeṅkum kāṇṭal aritaritu. taṅkaḷaik koṇṭē namatu civañāṉa tarmam iṉitu naṭaipeṟac ceytuvarum tiruvaruḷukku namatu vaṇakkam uriyatu eṉṟu ārāviruppār eḻutiṉār.
tiruvaraṅkar toṇṭarākavum puravalarākavum aṟivaṟinta aṉparākavum iruppatuṭaṉ tām peṟṟa aṟivunalattai mēṭaip poḻiviṉ vaḻiyē vāri vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷalākavum tikaḻa vēṇṭum eṉa aṭikaḷār eṇṇiṉār; mikamika virumpiṉār; ataṟkut tām utavum muyaṟciyum mēṟkoṇṭār. iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷukkumuṉ upanyāca viṣayam uṅkaḷukku aṉuppiyiruntēṉ. atu vantucērnta vivaram uṭaṉē teriviyuṅkaḷ. iṅṅaṉamē mātam ovvoṉṟu uṅkaṭku eḻutiyaṉuppuvēṉ. nīṅkaḷ tiruvaruṭṭiṟattāl naṉku upanniyacikkum āṟṟal peṟṟut tamiḻaiyum caivatti ṉaiyum eṅkum viḷaṅkac ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatutāṉ eṉatu peruṅ kōrikkai eṉṟu eḻuti vaḻikāṭṭiṉār. āṉāl araṅkar eṉṟum ceyaṟkaḷa vīrarākat tikaḻntārē yaṉṟic coṟkaḷa vīrarākat tōṉṟiṉār allar. oruvēḷai, collāṇmaiyiṉum ceyalāṇmaiyē aḻiyātatu eṉṟum, ceyalāṇmaiyil nilaipeṟuttāta collāṇmaiyāl payaṉētum illaiyē eṉṟum araṅkar uḷḷam tiṭṭap paṭuttiyirukkak kūṭum! ākaliṉ, araṅkar tam vāḻnāḷ muḻumaiyum coṟkaḷattil tammaip pukuttikkoṇṭār allar.
palapala coṟpoḻikaḷ naṭakka ēṟpāṭu ceytuvanta araṅkar tām coṟpoḻivu ceytalil nāṭṭañ celutta villai eṉiṉum, kaṭitam eḻututalil pērārvam koṇṭi runtār. kaṭitam eḻututalait taṉip peruṅ kalaiyākap pōṟṟiṉār. avartam eḻuttum aṭittal tiruttal iṉṟi muttukkōvai pōl ōr oḻuṅkupaṭa irukkum. tāḷiṉ mēlē kaiyai vaittu eḻutat toṭaṅkiṉāl oru ciṟu taṭaiyum illāmal appaṭiyē tāḷiṉ aṭippakuti varai viraintu eḻuti muṭikkum tiṟam araṅkarkku iyalpāy iruntatu. noṭip poḻutum, iṭaittaṭaiyuṟṟuk kaṭitam kaṇaṅkātu! kaṭitac ceytiyum, iḷamait tuṭippum, mutumait tiṟamum oruṅkē kāṭṭavallatāy amaintirukkum. payaṉaṟṟa ceyti oṉṟum avar kaṭitattil iṭam peṟuvatē illai.
iṉiya tampiyār va. cu. viṟku 14-8-1913il eḻutiya kaṭitattil kuṟippiṭukiṟār :
 nī iṉimēl tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷai ovvoru varaiyiṉ mēlum ilakkaṇa vaḻuviṉṟi fullstop, cama, semicolan, colan mutaliya puḷḷikaḷ pōṭa vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil pōṭṭum, vevvēṟu viṣayamākayiruntāl vevvēṟu pārāp pōṭṭe ḻutiyum eḻuttukkaḷ ovvoṉṟum muttu muttāyum taṉit taṉiyāyum kūṭṭeḻuttu eḻutāmalum eḻutap paḻaka vēṇṭum.
eḻuttukkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟi neṟippaṭuttum tiruvaraṅkar koḷḷattakka naṉṉeṟikaḷ cilavaṟṟaiyum kuṟippiṭṭu valiyuṟuttukiṟār :
nittiyappaṭi tēkāppiyācamum ceytuvara vēṇṭum. uṉ vēlaikaḷai Time Table pōṭṭu oḻuṅkāy (Regular) aṇukavum picakāmal naṭantu varuvāyeṉa nampukiṟēṉ. LibraryÆš News paper eṭuttu atai vācittu varavēṇṭum. atuvum viruttiyai uṇṭupaṇṇum.
“Om I am very active strong & healthy Sivayanama” itaṉai enta nāḷum kālaiyil 15 nimiṣam maṉatil niṉaittu orē cintaṉai ceytu varuvāyāṉāl nalla cukamum, palamum, mikunta ñāpaka caktiyum aṭaivāy. atāvatu taṉiyāka ōr aṟaiyil iruntu maṉattai ōrmaiyōṭu celutti uccāṭaṉam paṇṇik koṇṭu varuka.
tampiyār va. cu.vukku araṅkar itaṉai 14-8-1913il eḻutiṉār. appoḻutu tampiyār akavai 16. araṅkar akavai 23. ēḻāṇṭu mūtta tamaiyaṉārākavō araṅkar eḻutukiṉṟār? tantai nilaiyil iruntu kākkum, talaimakaṉā rākavaṉṟō eḻutukiṉṟār! tantaiyār maṟaiyumpōtu cuṭṭik kūṟiya kuṟippu avar uḷḷattiṉ āḻattuḷ āḻamāyp patintu kiṭantatu! nam peruṅ kuṭumpattait toṭakkamutal nāṉē pātukākkak kūṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu aiyar avarkaḷ iṟakkum pōtu cuṭṭik kāṭṭi aṟivuṟuttiya aṟivuraiyaic ciramēṟ koṇṭu tāmē kuṭumpak kaṭamaikaḷai aḷḷip pōṭṭukkoṇṭu araṅkar ceytārakaliṉ, tam cīriḷamaip paruvattilēyē ceḻumutiyarāy aṟivuṟutti vaḻikāṭṭum tiṟam peṟṟār eṉal amaiyum.


5. peruntakai vaḷḷal
tiruvaraṅkaṉār uḷḷattil aṭikaḷār kōyil koṇṭa nāḷ mutal, evvev vakaiyāl ellām avarkku utavalām eṉṟu tiṭṭamiṭṭukkoṇṭē iruntār. avvāṟu tiṭṭamiṭṭuc ceytavaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭaṉaic cuṭṭal cālum. oṉṟu tirucaṅkar kampeṉi eṉṉum peyarāl puttaka nilaiyam oṉṟu toṭaṅkiyatu; maṟṟoṉṟu aṭikaḷāriṉ āyvukku utavum vakaiyil tiṅkaḷ tōṟum oru tokai kiṭaikkumāṟu vakai ceytatu.
tiruvaraṅkariṉ keḻutakai naṇpar vi. caṅkara nārāyaṇapiḷḷai eṉpār. tam peyaraiyum curukki iṇaittut tirucaṅkar kampeṉi eṉṉum peyarāl 1917il oru puttaka niṟuvaṉattait toṭaṅkiṉār tiruvaraṅkar. ip puttaka niṟuvaṉam potuvāka aṉaittup puttakaṅkaḷaiyum vāṅki viṟṟu vantatu. eṉṟālum, aṭikaḷār nūlkaḷai ilaṅkaiyil peritum parappi aṭikaḷukku utavi ceytaṟkākavē tōṉṟiyatākum. piṉṉar in niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaḻiyākavē centamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉṉum peyarāl aṭikaḷār varainta tiruvācaka virivurai tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭāka veḷip paṭṭatu eṉpatum kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
tiruvaraṅkarum caṅkaranārāyaṇarum iṇaintu ceyta icceyal pōlavē, tiruvaraṅkarum avartam pēraṉpar 
rāma. pa. centilāṟumukam avarkaḷum iṇaintu ceyta utaviyē maṟṟoṉṟu ākum. aṭikaḷār nūlārāycci ceytal, purāṇaṅkaḷukku urai varaital ākiya ākkap paṇikaḷ ceyyumāṟu koḻumpu vaṇikap perumakkaḷiṭam tiṅkaḷvāri naṉkoṭait tiṭṭam oṉṟait toṭaṅki utaviṉār. ataṉpaṭi, 1918 mē varai rū. 1599-8-0 kiṭaittatu! ivvāṟu kālattāl utavum pēṟu tiruvaraṅkaṉārkku aṉṟi evarkkē vāyttatu? ataṉaip peṟṟu makiḻum pēṟum aṭikaḷārkku aṉṟi evarkkē vāyttatu? ivarkaḷ toṭarpē tiruvaruḷ toṭarpu pōlum!
īḻattu vāḻvaiyellām aṭikaḷār toṇṭukkē araṅkar āṭpaṭuttiviṭṭārō eṉṉum niṉaivu eḻumpalām. āṉāl araṅkar tām pukunta vaṇika niṟuvaṉak kaṭaṉai maṟappārō? tam kuṭumpak kaṭaṉai maṟappārō? tiruvaḷḷuvar aṟattuppāl oṉṟaṉōṭu amaiyāmal, poruṭpālum iṉpattuppālum ōtiya cīrmaiyai uṇarātavarō tiruvaraṅkar! muppālum pōṟṟi oḻukutalē muṟaimai eṉak koṇṭamaiyār, aṭikaḷārkku utavutalai aṟattup
pālāyt, tām pukuntu ceyyum vaṇikap paṇiyaip poruṭpālāyt, tam kuṭumpak kaṭamai puritalai iṉpattup pālāy mēṟkoṇṭu ovvoṉṟum kuṟaivuṟā vaṇṇam niṟaivuṟuttiṉār. vāy, pēciṉālum, kai, āṭi acaintālum, kaṇ, vaḻik kāṭciyaik kaṇṭālum, karuttellām talaimēl irukkum taṇṇīrkkuṭattiṉ mēlē irukkak kuṭaṅkoṇṭu nīr cumantu celvāraik kaṇṭuḷōm allamō! avvāṟu araṅkar avvak kaṭamaikaḷaip puritalil karuttākavē iruntār.
tikkaṟṟa nilaiyil irukkum tam kuṭumpattiṟkut tammāl iyaṉṟa aḷavum muyaṉṟu īṭṭiya poruḷaik kālam tavaṟāmal viṭuttu vantār. avar ceyta utaviyē aṉṉaiyārkku āṟutalāyiṟṟu! tam arumai makaṉār tam kuṭumpac cumaiyait tāṅkit takka vakaiyil uyarttiyē tīrvār eṉṉum muḻutuṟu nampikkaiyai ūṭṭi avarkku uvakai tantatu. uṭaṉ piṟanta iḷaiyāruḷ cuppaiyāviṉ paṭippil taṉi akkaṟai kāṭṭiṉār araṅkar. tantaiyār maṟaivāl, tam kalvi niṉṟuviṭṭatu pōl tam tampiyārkkum ākiviṭak kūṭātē eṉṟu añciṉār. ātalāl avar kalvikkut tām ceyya vēṇṭiya poruḷutavikaḷaiyellām taṭṭāmal viṭuttār. iḷaval cuppaiyāvukkē, tām kuṭumpattiṟku viṭukkum paṇattaiyellām viṭuttār. tiṭṭamiṭṭuc celaviṭal, cikkaṉattaip pēṇal, tavaṟātu varavu celavu eḻututal, kaṭamai tavaṟāmai eṉpavaṟṟai yellām ciṟṟiḷam paruvattilēyē iḷaval cuppaiyā eytutaṟkut tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyāy niṉṟu vaḻikāṭṭiṉār.
iḷaval va. cu. pāḷaiyil uḷḷa tūya cavēriyār uyarpaḷḷiyil payiṉṟār. 1916ām āṇṭu paḷḷiyiṟutit tērvil veṟṟi peṟṟār. appōtu vāyppāka irunta añcalakap paṇiyil cēra virumpiṉār. āṉāl aṇṇal tiruvaraṅkaṉār pēruḷḷam vēṟāka iruntatu. tampiyaik koḻumpukku varumāṟu eḻutiṉār. 1916ām āṇṭu cūlait tiṅkaḷil va. cu. koḻumpukkuc ceṉṟār.
koḻumpil irunta mutaṉilaik kallūriyil cērntār va.cu. ilaṇṭaṉ mānakara āciriyar kallūri naṭāttum tērviṉai eḻutuvataṟku ak kallūri payiṟci tantu vantatu. attērvukkuc ceṉṟu veṟṟi peṟṟār. maruttuvak kallūriyil cērtaṟkuriya vāyppu uṇṭu. ātalāl am muyaṟciyil ārvamāy ūṉṟutaṟku araṅkar ēṟpāṭu ceytār. tantaiyōṭu kalvipōm eṉpatai meyppittuviṭakkūṭātu eṉpatē araṅkariṉ kuṟikkōḷāka iruntatu.
ilaṅkaiyiṉ paruvanilai va. cu. viṉ uṭalnilaikku ēṟṟu varavillai. ātalāl, ōyāta nīrk kōvaikku āṭpaṭṭu allaluṟṟār. evvakaiyālum ataṉait tīrkka muṭiyāmaiyāl, ilaṅkaiyil iruntu pāḷaiyaṅkōṭṭaikkē tirumpiṉār.
pāḷaiyil iruntālum payilutalil peru vēṭkai yuṭaiya va. cu. tam payiṟciyai viṭṭār allar. vīṭṭil iruntu koṇṭē tērvukkuriya pāṭaṅkaḷait toṭarntu payiṉṟu tērvukkuc ceṉṟu, āṅku eḻutum muyaṟci mēṟkoṇṭār. avvāṟē uriya kālattil ceṉṟu tērvu eḻuti at tērvil veṟṟiyum peṟṟār.
ilaṇṭaṉ mānakarat tērvil peṟṟa veṟṟi, maruttuvak kallūriyil cēra ūkkiyatu. āṉāl, am muyaṟci veṟṟi taravillai. eṉiṉum tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil iṭainilai vakuppil cērntu toṭarntār. ivaṟṟuk
kellām tūṇṭalum tuṇaiyumāka iruntavarum ākkamum ūkkamum tantavarum araṅkarē eṉpataic colla vēṇṭiya
tillai. ākaliṉ, araṅkar camayappaṇi moḻippaṇikaḷil ūṉṟiyiruntārēṉum, tam kuṭumpattiṟkuc ceyyattakka paṇikaḷil karuttākavē iruntār eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
araṅkarkku iḷamaiyilēyē tokuppu ārvam mikkiruntatu. kiṭaittaṟku ariya nūlkaḷait tokuttal, cāṉṟōrkaḷ aṭiyārkaḷ paṭam tokuttal, uyarnta peru makkaḷiṉ kaṭitaṅkaḷ tokuttal iṉṉavāṟāṉa tokuppukaḷil iḷantaip paruvam toṭṭē pērārvam koṇṭiruntār. poruḷ muṭṭuppāṭu muṉṉiṉṟu takainta pōtumkūṭa arum poruḷkaḷai vilai tantu vāṅkic cērttalil karuttākavē iruntār araṅkar! avar piṉṉāḷil tokuppuk kalait tōṉṟalāyt tikaḻntataṟkum, tokupput tuṟaiyil iṇaiyaṟṟa tōṉṟalāyt tam tiruttampi cuppaiyāvait tikaḻac ceytataṟkum, mūlakkūṟu ilaṅkaiyil araṅkar irunta nāḷilēyē toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu eṉpatai ivaṇ niṉaivu kūrtal takum.



6. kātal arumputal
vaṇika niṟuvaṉap paṇikaḷiṉ iṭaiyē tuṇait toḻilāka aṉṟō araṅkar tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyai niṟuvik kaṭaṉāṟṟiṉār. ataṉ ceyaṟpāṭum payaṉ viḷaiyum araṅkar uḷḷattil ūṉṟiṉa. at toḻilaiyē tam vāḻvut toḻilāka koḷḷutal ciṟakkumeṉak karutiṉār. atē poḻutil tam vāḻvai tāyt tamiḻakattukku māṟṟik koḷvatu nalam eṉṟa muṭivukkum vantār. ataṟku aṭippaṭai aṭikaḷārkku aṇukkamākiyiruntu avar paṇikku ollum vakaiyāl ellām utavakkūṭum eṉpatākavē iruntatu. ākaliṉ tirucaṅkar kampeṉiyaic ceṉṉaiyil niṟuvic ceyalāṟṟutaṟkut tuṇintār. at tuṇiviṟku neñciṉ aṭittaḷattil ōr uṇarvum iruntatu pōlum!
tiruvaraṅkaṉāriṉ keḻutakai naṇpar centil āṟumukam avarkaḷaippaṟṟi muṉṉarē aṟivōm. avar orukāl pallāvarattiṟku vantu aṭikaḷāraik kaṇṭu aḷavaḷāvic ceṉṟār. ceṉṟavar aṭikaḷāriṉ aṉpu māṇpaiyum, avar tam illattār viruntōmpu talaiyum, aḻakē vaṭiveṭuttu aṟivāṉ mutirntu viḷaṅkum nīlāmpikai eṉṉum aṭikaḷār tam arumaic celviyar taṉip perum cīrmaiyaiyum, aṭikaḷār am makaḷār māṭṭuk koṇṭuḷḷa taṉip pēraṉpaiyum, iṉṉa piṟavaṟṟaiyum araṅkar tiruc ceviyil cērttiruntār. araṅkar uḷḷattil nīlāmpikai eṉṉum poṟkoṭi miṉṉaliṭat toṭaṅkiyatu. ‘mo fsh®¡F m‹ò kUfuhŒ mikí« ngW thŒ¡Fnkh! eṉṟu tamakkuḷ viṉāvi, aṭikaḷār kāṭṭi varum pēraṉpup peru veḷḷap perukkil nīntum pēṟu vāytta eḷiyēṉ marukarām pēṟu eytalum kūṭuvatē eṉṟu amaintu tāyt tamiḻakam nōkkiṉār.
tiruvaraṅkar tamiḻakattil nilaiyāyt taṅkum tiṭṭattuṭaṉ vantār. koḻumpil iruntu tūttukkuṭikku vantu pāḷaiyaṅ kōṭṭaikkuc ceṉṟār. tam arumai aṉṉaiyāraiyum, uṭaṉ piṟantārkaḷaiyum kaṇṭu uvakai kūrntār. ciṉṉāḷkaḷ ceṉṟu, ceṉṉaikkup puṟappaṭṭār. tam mēṟkoṇṭa toḻilpaṟṟi aṭikaḷāriṭam kalantu uraiyāṭa vēṇṭumē! avar karuttaṟintu niṟuvaṉattait toṭaṅki naṭatta vēṇṭumē! nērē pallāvarattiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṭikaḷārai avartam vaḷamaṉaiyil kaṇṭār. kaṇṭa kāṭci eppaṭi? varavēṟpu eppaṭi? uṭaṉiruntu kaṇṭa aṭikaḷār celvar uraikkiṟār :
aṭikaḷiṉ nalvāḻviṟku uyar toṇṭiṟku uyirāy niṉṟu utaviya vāṉaṉaiya vaḷḷalār tiruvaraṅkam oru nāṭkālaip poḻutu (9-11-1918) nāṅkaḷ yāvarum ārā vēṭkaiyuṭaṉ etirpārttu iruntapaṭi pallāvaram vantu cērntār. avarai varavēṟṟu makiḻvatil eṅkaḷ kuṭumpam apparai varavēṟṟa appūtiyaṭikaḷ kuṭumpattaiyum miñciviṭṭatu. tiruvaraṅkaraik kaṇṭu nāṅkaḷ aṭainta makiḻccikku ellaiyillai. avaraip pōṟṟuvatil oruvark koruvar muntiṉōm. aṭiyārkku aṭiyārāka yārum virumpuvar allarō! eṉ aṉpāṉa peṟṟōrkaḷ avarukku vakai vakaiyāṉa viruntūṭṭi makiḻntaṉar. tiruvaraṅkarukku appōtu āṇṭu 28. paranta tirumukamum, tirunīṟṟiṉāl niṟainta paranta neṟṟiyum, akaṉṟa peru viḻikaḷum, uyarntakaṉṟa mārpum, kallaṉaiya tōḷum tāḷum koṇṭu kaṭṭamainta kāḷaiyāk kaviṉ poḻiyum mēṉiyarāyt tiruvaraṅkar ilaṅkiṉār. (maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ varalāṟu. 324)
nīlāmpikaiyārkku appoḻutu akavai patiṉāṟu. iḷamaiyiṉ vaḷamum aṟivuṉ polivum koṇṭu eḻilmiku naṅkaiyāy viḷaṅkiṉār. nākaiyaic cārntavar allarō aṭikaḷār? ātalāl nākaiyil kōyil koṇṭa ammaiyiṉ tiruppeyarait tam makaḷukkuc cūṭṭiṉār. nīlāmpikaiyār ñu6-8-1903il piṟantār.
nīlā toṭakkap paḷḷip paṭippai ceṉṉaiyilum pallāvarattilum payiṉṟār. pallāvarattil ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vakuppup paḷḷi ak kālattil illāmaiyāl avar paḷḷip paṭippu avvaḷavil niṉṟatu!
paṭippu, paḷḷiyil maṭṭum payilap peṟuvatō? ataṟku iṭamētu? kālamētu? ārvam iruntāl eṅkum eṉṟum kaṟka vāyppu uṇṭē! ulakattaip pōlat tiṟantaveḷip paḷḷik kūṭam oṉṟu uṇṭō? atilum aṭikaḷārtam aṉpuc celviyiṉ kalvikkup paḷḷikkūṭam viṭuttamai oru taṭaiyāmō? mummoḻi valla aṭikaḷār pulamai nalam ellām nīlāviṉiṭattut taṅkiyatu. avar tāmum mummoḻip pulamaiyum, ilakkiya ilakkaṇat tērcciyum, tirumuṟai tirumuṟait tiṟamum oruṅkē vāykkap peṟṟār. aṭikaḷār, tam makkaḷuḷ nīlāmpikaiyārkkē ārvattoṭum kaṟpittār eṉpatum, palvēṟu paṇikaḷukku iṭaiyēyum kālamum - kaḷamum vakuttu muṟaiyē kaṟpittār eṉpatum karutattak
kaṉavām.
aṭikaḷiṉ kuralō tēṉiṉum iṉiyatām! nīlāmpikaiyiṉ kuralō ataṉiṉum iṉiyatām! yāḻum, kuḻalum, kuyilum nīlāmpikaiyiṉ kuralukkut tiṟai celuttal vēṇṭumām! aṭikaḷ tōṭi, pairavi, āṉanta pairavi, mōkaṉam, mattiyamāvati, caṅkarāparaṇam, nīlāmpari mutalāya icaikaḷai nīlāmpikaikkuk kaṟpittārām!
1916ām āṇṭu! nīlāvukku akavai 13! aṭikaḷār vaḷḷalār pāṭiya,
